## scripts/check_data_pipeline.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Check the STRATUM real-data ingestion pipeline end to end.

Looks for the ERA5 analysis file ``era5_del_bom_20250101.nc`` in the
project root, a ``data/`` directory, and the current working directory.
When the file is present, ambient temperature and humidity are
interpolated along a simulated FL350 Delhi-Mumbai route and the
Schmidt-Appleman criterion is evaluated at every waypoint. When the file
is missing, the same check runs on an in-memory synthetic mock dataset
and prints download guidance.

Exit code is 0 when the route evaluation completes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
import warnings
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import xarray as xr

# The netCDF4 1.7.2 wheel can emit a spurious NumPy binary-compatibility
# RuntimeWarning on first engine use; functionality is exercised below.
warnings.filterwarnings(
    "ignore",
    message="numpy.ndarray size changed, may indicate binary incompatibility",
    category=RuntimeWarning,
)

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.io.era5 import (  # noqa: E402
    ERA5_PRESSURE_LEVELS_HPA,
    interpolate_met_to_segment,
    load_era5_dataset,
)
from stratum.physics import e_i, evaluate_sac  # noqa: E402
from stratum.routes.corridor import AIRPORTS, great_circle_waypoints  # noqa: E402

ERA5_FILENAME = "era5_del_bom_20250101.nc"
CRUISE_FL = 350.0
N_WAYPOINTS = 7
EPS = 0.622


def _candidate_paths() -> list[Path]:
    """Locations searched for the real ERA5 file, in order."""
    return [
        PROJECT_ROOT / ERA5_FILENAME,
        PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / ERA5_FILENAME,
        Path.cwd() / ERA5_FILENAME,
    ]


def _find_era5_file() -> Path | None:
    """Return the first existing candidate path, or ``None``."""
    for candidate in _candidate_paths():
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
    return None


def _rhi_from_specific_humidity(
    specific_humidity: float, pressure_pa: float, temperature_k: float
) -> float:
    """Convert ERA5 specific humidity to relative humidity over ice.

    Vapor pressure follows ``e = q p / (eps + (1 - eps) q)``; RHi divides
    by the Murphy & Koop ice saturation pressure.
    """
    vapor_pressure = specific_humidity * pressure_pa / (
        EPS + (1.0 - EPS) * specific_humidity
    )
    return vapor_pressure / e_i(temperature_k)


def _synthetic_era5() -> xr.Dataset:
    """Build a physically plausible mock ERA5 dataset over the domain.

    Temperature decreases with pressure along a fixed lapse; the specific
    humidity field is built by inverting the RHi definition for a
    wavenumber-like winter field between RHi 0.15 and 1.4, so the mock
    spans both forming and non-forming (and supersaturated) points.
    """
    lats = np.array([15.0, 18.0, 21.0, 24.0, 27.0, 30.0, 32.0])
    lons = np.array([68.0, 71.0, 74.0, 77.0, 80.0, 82.0])
    levels = np.array(ERA5_PRESSURE_LEVELS_HPA, dtype=float)
    times = np.array(
        ["2025-01-01T00:00", "2025-01-01T06:00"], dtype="datetime64[ns]"
    )

    level = levels[:, None, None, None]
    lat = lats[None, :, None, None]
    lon = lons[None, None, :, None]
    t_index = np.arange(times.size, dtype=float)[None, None, None, :]

    temperature = (
        216.65
        + 0.11 * (level - 200.0)
        + 0.0 * lat
        + 0.0 * lon
        + 0.0 * t_index
    )
    target_rhi = np.clip(
        0.5
        + 0.6
        * np.sin(np.pi * (lat - 15.0) / 17.0)
        * np.cos(np.pi * (lon - 68.0) / 14.0),
        0.15,
        1.4,
    )
    saturation_pressure = np.array(
        [e_i(float(t)) for t in (216.65 + 0.11 * (levels - 200.0))]
    )[:, None, None, None]
    specific_humidity = (
        target_rhi * saturation_pressure * EPS / (level * 100.0)
    ) * (1.0 + 0.05 * t_index)

    u_wind = 8.0 + 0.05 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.0 * t_index + 0.0 * level + 0.0 * lon
    v_wind = -3.0 + 0.1 * (lon - 68.0) + 0.0 * t_index + 0.0 * level + 0.0 * lat
    omega = 0.002 + 0.0 * level + 0.0 * lat + 0.0 * lon + 0.0 * t_index
    cloud = 0.3 + 0.0 * level + 0.0 * lat + 0.0 * lon + 0.0 * t_index

    fields = {
        "temperature": temperature,
        "specific_humidity": specific_humidity,
        "u_component_of_wind": u_wind,
        "v_component_of_wind": v_wind,
        "vertical_velocity": omega,
        "fraction_of_cloud_cover": cloud,
    }
    data_vars = {
        name: (("time", "level", "latitude", "longitude"), field.transpose(3, 0, 1, 2))
        for name, field in fields.items()
    }
    return xr.Dataset(
        data_vars,
        coords={
            "time": times,
            "level": levels,
            "latitude": lats,
            "longitude": lons,
        },
    )


def _evaluate_route(
    ds_met: xr.Dataset,
    source_label: str,
    time_value: Any,
) -> int:
    """Evaluate the SAC along the simulated route; return the exit code."""
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS["VIDP"], AIRPORTS["VABB"], num_points=N_WAYPOINTS, fl=CRUISE_FL
    )
    query_time = np.datetime_as_string(np.datetime64(time_value, "s"))
    print(f"Data source:  {source_label}")
    print(
        f"Route:        VIDP (Delhi) -> VABB (Mumbai), cruise FL{CRUISE_FL:.0f}, "
        f"{len(waypoints)} waypoints"
    )
    print(f"Query time:   {query_time} UTC")
    print()
    header = (
        f"{'#':>2} {'lat':>7} {'lon':>7} {'p [hPa]':>8} {'T [C]':>7} "
        f"{'RHi':>6} {'TLM [C]':>8} {'TLC [C]':>8} {'forms':>6} {'persists':>8}"
    )
    print(header)
    print("-" * len(header))

    sac_true = 0
    persists_count = 0
    for index, waypoint in enumerate(waypoints, start=1):
        values = interpolate_met_to_segment(
            ds_met,
            lat=waypoint["lat"],
            lon=waypoint["lon"],
            pressure_pa=waypoint["p_Pa"],
            time_dt=time_value,
        )
        temperature = values["temperature"]
        humidity = values["specific_humidity"]
        if not (math.isfinite(temperature) and math.isfinite(humidity)):
            print(f"{index:>2}  no data inside the dataset domain")
            continue
        rhi = _rhi_from_specific_humidity(humidity, waypoint["p_Pa"], temperature)
        sac = evaluate_sac(waypoint["p_Pa"], temperature, rhi)
        sac_true += int(sac["forms"])
        persists_count += int(sac["persists"])
        print(
            f"{index:>2} {waypoint['lat']:>7.2f} {waypoint['lon']:>7.2f} "
            f"{waypoint['p_Pa'] / 100.0:>8.1f} {temperature - 273.15:>7.2f} "
            f"{rhi:>6.2f} {sac['TLM_C']:>8.2f} {sac['TLC_C']:>8.2f} "
            f"{str(sac['forms']):>6} {str(sac['persists']):>8}"
        )

    print()
    print(
        f"SAC holds at {sac_true}/{len(waypoints)} waypoints; "
        f"ice-supersaturated at {persists_count}/{len(waypoints)}."
    )
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    real_path = _find_era5_file()
    if real_path is not None:
        ds_met = load_era5_dataset(str(real_path))
        return _evaluate_route(
            ds_met,
            f"real ERA5 file {real_path}",
            ds_met["time"].values[0],
        )

    print(f"NOTE: real ERA5 file '{ERA5_FILENAME}' not found.")
    print("Searched:")
    for candidate in _candidate_paths():
        print(f"  - {candidate}")
    print()
    print("Running the same check against an in-memory synthetic ERA5 mock instead.")
    print("To use real data, download the ERA5 file with the CDS API and place it at:")
    print(f"  {PROJECT_ROOT / 'data' / ERA5_FILENAME}")
    print()
    ds_met = _synthetic_era5()
    return _evaluate_route(
        ds_met,
        "synthetic mock (no ERA5 file on disk)",
        np.datetime64("2025-01-01T03:00"),
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## scripts/demo_decide.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Demo: counterfactual decision layer (Project STRATUM, Step 6).

Simulates a flagged Delhi-Mumbai (VIDP-VABB) flight at FL350 with a
calibrated contrail persistence probability of 0.78. Evaluates the two
primary counterfactual flight levels (FL370 step climb, FL330 descent)
and prints an auditable decision card with fuel, CO2, avoided contrail
energy forcing, final status, and a dispatcher recommendation.

Exit code is 0 when the scenario yields an eligible recommendation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.decide import (  # noqa: E402
    AltitudeDecision,
    CandidateEvaluation,
    evaluate_altitude_candidates,
)

FLIGHT_LABEL = "VIDP (Delhi) -> VABB (Mumbai)"
AIRCRAFT_TYPE = "A320"
BASELINE_FL = 350.0
BASELINE_P_PERSIST = 0.78


def _format_candidate(candidate: CandidateEvaluation) -> str:
    """Render one auditable candidate row."""
    label = f"FL{candidate['target_fl']:.0f}"
    change = f"{candidate['direction']} {candidate['altitude_change_ft']:+.0f} ft"
    if candidate["rejected"]:
        return f"{label}  {change:<18}: REJECT ({candidate['rejection_reason']})"
    verdict = "ELIGIBLE" if candidate["net_benefit_positive"] else "REJECT"
    avoided = candidate["avoided_ef_j"]
    return (
        f"{label}  {change:<18}: fuel +{candidate['delta_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg | "
        f"CO2 +{candidate['delta_co2_kg']:.2f} kg | "
        f"avoided warming {avoided:.2e} J ({avoided / 1.0e9:.2f} GJ) | "
        f"benefit/cost {candidate['benefit_to_cost_ratio']:.2e} | {verdict}"
    )


def _dispatcher_recommendation(decision: AltitudeDecision) -> str:
    """Human-readable recommendation for the dispatcher."""
    baseline_fl = decision["baseline_fl"]
    status = decision["status"]
    if status == "abstain":
        return (
            f"No action at FL{baseline_fl:.0f}: persistence probability "
            f"{decision['baseline_p_persist']:.2f} is below the 0.40 activation "
            "threshold, so extra fuel is not justified."
        )
    if status == "no_robust_candidate":
        return (
            f"Keep FL{baseline_fl:.0f}: no counterfactual flight level passes the "
            "benefit test under the current constraints."
        )

    recommended = decision["recommended_fl"]
    delta_fuel = decision["delta_fuel_kg"]
    delta_co2 = decision["delta_co2_kg"]
    avoided = decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"]
    if recommended is None or delta_fuel is None or delta_co2 is None or avoided is None:
        raise RuntimeError("eligible decision is missing recommendation fields")
    direction = "step climb" if recommended > baseline_fl else "descent"
    return (
        f"Recommend {direction} from FL{baseline_fl:.0f} to FL{recommended:.0f} for the "
        f"flagged segment: avoids {avoided:.2e} J of contrail energy forcing for an "
        f"extra {delta_fuel:.1f} kg of fuel ({delta_co2:.1f} kg CO2). Coordinate with "
        "ATC and confirm fuel reserves before the level change."
    )


def main() -> int:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(
        baseline_p_persist=BASELINE_P_PERSIST,
        baseline_fl=BASELINE_FL,
        aircraft_type=AIRCRAFT_TYPE,
    )

    print("STRATUM Step 6 - Counterfactual Decision Layer demo")
    print("=" * 66)
    print(f"Flight: {FLIGHT_LABEL}, aircraft {AIRCRAFT_TYPE}")
    print(
        f"Flagged segment at FL{BASELINE_FL:.0f} with calibrated "
        f"P(persist) = {BASELINE_P_PERSIST:.2f} (activation threshold 0.40)"
    )
    print()
    print("Counterfactual candidates")
    print("-" * 66)
    for candidate in decision["candidates"]:
        print(_format_candidate(candidate))
    print()
    print("Decision card")
    print("-" * 66)
    print(f"Baseline flight level:      FL{decision['baseline_fl']:.0f}")
    print(f"Calibrated P(persist):      {decision['baseline_p_persist']:.2f}")
    recommended = decision["recommended_fl"]
    if recommended is not None:
        delta_fl = recommended - decision["baseline_fl"]
        direction = "step climb" if delta_fl > 0.0 else "descent"
        print(f"Recommended flight level:   FL{recommended:.0f} ({direction})")
        print(f"Delta fuel:                 +{decision['delta_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg")
        print(f"Delta CO2:                  +{decision['delta_co2_kg']:.2f} kg")
        avoided = decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"]
        print(f"Avoided warming:            {avoided:.2e} J ({avoided / 1.0e9:.2f} GJ)")
        print(f"Benefit-to-cost ratio:      {decision['benefit_to_cost_ratio']:.2e}")
    else:
        print("Recommended flight level:   none")
    print(f"Final status:               {decision['status'].upper()}")
    print(f"Reason: {decision['reason']}")
    print()
    print(f"Dispatcher recommendation: {_dispatcher_recommendation(decision)}")
    return 0 if decision["status"] == "eligible" else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## scripts/demo_detector.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Demo of the classical satellite contrail detector on a synthetic scene.

Builds a 160x160 split-window scene at 240 K with Gaussian noise that
contains a clear-sky background, one straight contrail (60 pixels at
+2.5 K BTD) and one round cirrus blob (radius 10 pixels at +3.0 K BTD),
runs :func:`stratum.detect.filters.detect_contrail_lines`, and prints a
short auditable report confirming detection of the contrail and
rejection of the blob.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.detect.filters import detect_contrail_lines  # noqa: E402

SEED = 0
SCENE_SIZE = 160
BACKGROUND_K = 240.0
NOISE_STD_K = 0.25
CONTRAIL_ANGLE_DEG = 35.0
CONTRAIL_LENGTH_PX = 60
CONTRAIL_ANOMALY_K = 2.5
BLOB_CENTRE = (120.0, 110.0)
BLOB_RADIUS_PX = 10
BLOB_ANOMALY_K = 3.0
ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG = 15.0


def build_scene(seed: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Return a synthetic (bt108, bt120) scene with a contrail and a blob."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    bt108 = BACKGROUND_K + rng.normal(0.0, NOISE_STD_K, (SCENE_SIZE, SCENE_SIZE))
    bt120 = BACKGROUND_K + rng.normal(0.0, NOISE_STD_K, (SCENE_SIZE, SCENE_SIZE))

    start_col = 30
    cols = np.arange(start_col, start_col + CONTRAIL_LENGTH_PX)
    rows = np.round(30.0 + (cols - start_col) * math.tan(math.radians(CONTRAIL_ANGLE_DEG))).astype(int)
    bt108[np.clip(rows, 0, SCENE_SIZE - 1), cols] += CONTRAIL_ANOMALY_K

    grid_y, grid_x = np.mgrid[0:SCENE_SIZE, 0:SCENE_SIZE]
    blob_mask = (
        (grid_y - BLOB_CENTRE[0]) ** 2 + (grid_x - BLOB_CENTRE[1]) ** 2
        <= BLOB_RADIUS_PX**2
    )
    bt108[blob_mask] += BLOB_ANOMALY_K
    return bt108, bt120


def _angular_distance(angle_a: float, angle_b: float) -> float:
    """Return the smallest angle difference in [0, 90] degrees."""
    difference = abs(angle_a - angle_b) % 180.0
    return min(difference, 180.0 - difference)


def main() -> int:
    """Run the detector demo and print the summary report."""
    bt108, bt120 = build_scene(SEED)
    result = detect_contrail_lines(bt108, bt120)
    objects = result["objects"]

    print("Project STRATUM - classical satellite contrail detector demo")
    print(
        f"Scene {SCENE_SIZE}x{SCENE_SIZE} px, {BACKGROUND_K:.0f} K background, "
        f"Gaussian noise sigma={NOISE_STD_K:.2f} K (seed {SEED})"
    )
    print(
        f"Planted contrail: {CONTRAIL_LENGTH_PX} px at {CONTRAIL_ANGLE_DEG:.1f} deg, "
        f"+{CONTRAIL_ANOMALY_K:.1f} K BTD"
    )
    print(
        f"Planted cloud blob: radius {BLOB_RADIUS_PX} px at {BLOB_CENTRE}, "
        f"+{BLOB_ANOMALY_K:.1f} K BTD"
    )
    print()

    if objects:
        print("| Object | Centroid (row, col) | Length px | Angle deg | Mean BTD K |")
        print("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
        for index, obj in enumerate(objects, start=1):
            centroid = obj["centroid"]
            print(
                f"| {index} | ({centroid[0]:.1f}, {centroid[1]:.1f}) | "
                f"{obj['length_px']:.1f} | {obj['angle_deg']:.1f} | {obj['mean_btd']:.3f} |"
            )
    else:
        print("No objects detected.")
    print()

    contrail_hits = [
        obj
        for obj in objects
        if _angular_distance(obj["angle_deg"], CONTRAIL_ANGLE_DEG) <= ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG
    ]
    blob_hits = [
        obj
        for obj in objects
        if math.hypot(
            obj["centroid"][0] - BLOB_CENTRE[0], obj["centroid"][1] - BLOB_CENTRE[1]
        )
        <= BLOB_RADIUS_PX + 6.0
    ]
    contrail_detected = len(contrail_hits) >= 1
    blob_rejected = len(blob_hits) == 0

    print(
        f"Contrail detected: {'YES' if contrail_detected else 'NO'} "
        f"({len(contrail_hits)} object(s) within +/-{ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG:.0f} deg "
        f"of {CONTRAIL_ANGLE_DEG:.1f} deg)"
    )
    print(
        f"Round cloud blob rejected: {'YES' if blob_rejected else 'NO'} "
        f"({len(blob_hits)} detection(s) near the blob centre)"
    )
    print(f"Total objects after length/aspect gates: {result['num_detected']}")

    return 0 if (contrail_detected and blob_rejected) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## scripts/demo_matching.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Demo of flight-contrail association with parallax and tri-state labels.

Simulates a flight segment on the Delhi-Mumbai corridor at FL350,
advects it 45 minutes downwind with a 30 m/s westerly jet, applies the
Meteosat-9 parallax correction, matches it against a synthetic satellite
detection, and demonstrates the tri-state labels for a clear-sky match,
a clear-sky non-detection and an unobservable storm scene.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.match.advect import advect_segment  # noqa: E402
from stratum.match.label import (  # noqa: E402
    assign_tristate_label,
    haversine_km,
    match_segment_to_detections,
)
from stratum.match.observe import is_scene_observable  # noqa: E402
from stratum.match.parallax import calculate_parallax_displacement  # noqa: E402
from stratum.routes.corridor import AIRPORTS, great_circle_waypoints  # noqa: E402

FL = 350.0
SEGMENT_WAYPOINT_INDEX = 10
ADVECTION_SECONDS = 45.0 * 60.0
U_JET_MPS = 30.0  # westerly jet: eastward
V_JET_MPS = 0.0
ALTITUDE_M = 35000.0 * 0.3048
DISTANCE_TOLERANCE_KM = 10.0
ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG = 20.0
TOTAL_SLOTS = 3
DETECTION_OFFSET_DEG = 0.005  # ~0.5 km north of the apparent position


def _initial_bearing_deg(
    coord1: tuple[float, float], coord2: tuple[float, float]
) -> float:
    """Initial great-circle bearing from coord1 to coord2 [deg, 0-360]."""
    lat1, lat2 = math.radians(coord1[0]), math.radians(coord2[0])
    delta_lon = math.radians(coord2[1] - coord1[1])
    x = math.sin(delta_lon) * math.cos(lat2)
    y = math.cos(lat1) * math.sin(lat2) - math.sin(lat1) * math.cos(lat2) * math.cos(
        delta_lon
    )
    return math.degrees(math.atan2(x, y)) % 360.0


def main() -> int:
    """Run the matching demo and print the summary tables."""
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS["VIDP"], AIRPORTS["VABB"], num_points=25, fl=FL
    )
    current = waypoints[SEGMENT_WAYPOINT_INDEX]
    following = waypoints[SEGMENT_WAYPOINT_INDEX + 1]
    segment_coord = (current["lat"], current["lon"])
    heading_deg = _initial_bearing_deg(
        segment_coord, (following["lat"], following["lon"])
    )

    advected = advect_segment(
        segment_coord[0], segment_coord[1], U_JET_MPS, V_JET_MPS, ADVECTION_SECONDS
    )
    drift_km = haversine_km(segment_coord, advected)
    delta_lat, delta_lon = calculate_parallax_displacement(
        advected[0], advected[1], ALTITUDE_M
    )
    apparent = (advected[0] + delta_lat, advected[1] + delta_lon)
    parallax_km = haversine_km(advected, apparent)

    detection = {
        "centroid": (apparent[0] + DETECTION_OFFSET_DEG, apparent[1]),
        "angle_deg": heading_deg % 180.0,
        "length_px": 42.0,
        "mean_btd": 1.8,
    }

    print("Project STRATUM - flight-contrail association demo")
    print(
        f"Segment: VIDP-VABB waypoint {SEGMENT_WAYPOINT_INDEX} at FL{FL:.0f} "
        f"(lat {segment_coord[0]:.4f}, lon {segment_coord[1]:.4f}), "
        f"heading {heading_deg:.1f} deg"
    )
    print(
        f"Advection: 45 min at {U_JET_MPS:.0f} m/s westerly -> drift {drift_km:.1f} km "
        f"(lat {advected[0]:.4f}, lon {advected[1]:.4f})"
    )
    print(
        f"Meteosat-9 parallax: delta_lat {delta_lat:+.4f} deg, "
        f"delta_lon {delta_lon:+.4f} deg ({parallax_km:.1f} km) -> "
        f"apparent (lat {apparent[0]:.4f}, lon {apparent[1]:.4f})"
    )
    print(
        f"Synthetic detection: centroid offset "
        f"{haversine_km(apparent, detection['centroid']):.1f} km, "
        f"angle {detection['angle_deg']:.1f} deg"
    )
    print()

    scenarios: list[tuple[str, str, float, float, list[dict[str, object]], bool]] = [
        (
            "1",
            "Clear sky, matching line",
            0.05,
            32.0,
            [detection],
            True,
        ),
        (
            "2",
            "Clear sky, no line",
            0.05,
            32.0,
            [],
            True,
        ),
        (
            "3",
            "Stormy/cloudy sky, no line",
            0.85,
            32.0,
            [],
            False,
        ),
    ]

    print(
        "| Scenario | Sky condition | Observable slots | Matched | Label | Interpretation |"
    )
    print("| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")

    expected_labels: list[float] = []
    actual_labels: list[float] = []
    for name, sky, cloud_fraction, zenith, detections, expected_observable in scenarios:
        observable = is_scene_observable(cloud_fraction, zenith)
        assert observable is expected_observable
        observable_slots = TOTAL_SLOTS if observable else 0
        matched = match_segment_to_detections(
            apparent,
            heading_deg,
            detections,
            distance_tolerance_km=DISTANCE_TOLERANCE_KM,
            angle_tolerance_deg=ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG,
        )
        label_value = assign_tristate_label(matched, observable_slots)
        if math.isnan(label_value):
            interpretation = "Unknown/unobservable (NaN, not a negative)"
            label_text = "NaN"
        elif label_value == 1.0:
            interpretation = "Positive: contrail detected on segment"
            label_text = "1.0"
        else:
            interpretation = "Observable non-detection"
            label_text = "0.0"
        print(
            f"| {name} | {sky} | {observable_slots} | {matched} | "
            f"{label_text} | {interpretation} |"
        )
        expected = 1.0 if name == "1" else (0.0 if name == "2" else float("nan"))
        expected_labels.append(expected)
        actual_labels.append(label_value)

    ok = all(
        (math.isnan(exp) and math.isnan(act)) or exp == act
        for exp, act in zip(expected_labels, actual_labels)
    )
    print()
    print(
        "Tri-state labels as expected: "
        f"{'YES' if ok else 'NO'} (1.0 positive, 0.0 observable non-detection, "
        "NaN unobservable/unknown)"
    )
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## scripts/demo_model.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Demo: physics-constrained correction model (Project STRATUM, Step 5).

Generates a synthetic dataset of flight segments with physically plausible
atmospheric states, injects tri-state labels (0/1 with sensor noise, NaN
for unobservable scenes), trains a monotone LightGBM classifier, fits
isotonic calibration on the validation split, and reports train/validation
counts, raw versus calibrated Brier scores, a monotonicity verification,
and sample predictions for cold/humid versus warm/dry states.

Exit code is 0 when both monotonicity checks pass.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
import warnings
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.model import (  # noqa: E402
    CalibratedModel,
    FEATURE_NAMES,
    compute_brier_score,
    extract_features_row,
    feature_vector,
    fit_calibrator,
    train_contrail_model,
)
from stratum.physics import (  # noqa: E402
    G,
    critical_T,
    e_i,
    e_w,
    fl_to_p,
    solve_TLM,
)

warnings.filterwarnings(
    "ignore",
    message=(
        "X does not have valid feature names, but LGBMClassifier was "
        "fitted with feature names"
    ),
)

N_SEGMENTS = 2000
TRAIN_FRACTION = 0.7
UNOBSERVABLE_FRACTION = 0.2
LABEL_NOISE_FRACTION = 0.05
RANDOM_SEED = 42
TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M = 11000.0

RHI_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi")
TLM_K_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("tlm_k")


def _generate_dataset(rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Build synthetic segments with physics-based tri-state labels.

    Each segment samples a flight level and derives pressure and the
    Schmidt-Appleman thresholds from :mod:`stratum.physics`. The label is
    drawn from a latent risk that increases with ice supersaturation and
    decreases with the temperature margin, then flipped with sensor noise.
    Unobservable segments (opaque cloud) get a NaN label.
    """
    rows: list[np.ndarray] = []
    labels: list[float] = []
    for _ in range(N_SEGMENTS):
        flight_level = rng.uniform(300.0, 400.0)
        pressure_pa = fl_to_p(flight_level) * 100.0
        altitude_m = flight_level * 100.0 * 0.3048
        tlm_k = solve_TLM(pressure_pa)
        temp_margin_tlm = rng.uniform(-12.0, 4.0)
        temp_k = tlm_k + temp_margin_tlm
        rhi = float(rng.beta(2.0, 2.0)) * 1.7
        rhw = rhi * e_i(temp_k) / e_w(temp_k)
        tlc_k = critical_T(rhw, tlm_k, G(pressure_pa))

        rhi_above = float(np.clip(rhi + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12), 0.0, 2.0))
        rhi_below = float(np.clip(rhi + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12), 0.0, 2.0))

        unobservable = rng.random() < UNOBSERVABLE_FRACTION
        if unobservable:
            high_cloud_fraction = float(rng.uniform(0.6, 0.95))
        else:
            high_cloud_fraction = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.6))

        richardson_number = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 4.0))
        solar_zenith_deg = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 180.0))
        engine_efficiency_eta = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.40))
        log_soot_ei_n = float(rng.uniform(14.0, 16.0))

        logit = (
            2.4 * (rhi - 0.85)
            - 0.35 * temp_margin_tlm
            - 1.5 * high_cloud_fraction
            - 0.5 * (richardson_number - 1.0)
            + 4.0 * (engine_efficiency_eta - 0.32)
        )
        persistent = rng.random() < 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-logit))
        if rng.random() < LABEL_NOISE_FRACTION:
            persistent = not persistent
        label = float("nan") if unobservable else float(persistent)

        lon = float(rng.uniform(68.0, 90.0))
        utc_hours = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 24.0))
        dist_to_tropopause_m = (
            altitude_m - TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M + float(rng.normal(0.0, 150.0))
        )
        row = extract_features_row(
            rhi=rhi,
            rhi_above=rhi_above,
            rhi_below=rhi_below,
            rhi_grad=rhi_above - rhi_below,
            temp_k=temp_k,
            tlm_k=tlm_k,
            tlc_k=tlc_k,
            pressure_pa=pressure_pa,
            cloud_ice=float(rng.random() < 0.25),
            high_cloud_fraction=high_cloud_fraction,
            vertical_velocity_omega=float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08)),
            vertical_wind_shear=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 15.0)),
            dist_to_tropopause_m=dist_to_tropopause_m,
            lat=float(rng.uniform(8.0, 30.0)),
            lon=lon,
            month=float(rng.integers(1, 13)),
            local_solar_time_hours=(utc_hours + lon / 15.0) % 24.0,
            richardson_number=richardson_number,
            solar_zenith_deg=solar_zenith_deg,
            engine_efficiency_eta=engine_efficiency_eta,
            log_soot_ei_n=log_soot_ei_n,
        )
        rows.append(feature_vector(row))
        labels.append(label)
    return np.vstack(rows), np.asarray(labels, dtype=float)


def _sweep(
    model: CalibratedModel,
    baseline: np.ndarray,
    index: int,
    values: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Calibrated positive-class probabilities along one feature sweep."""
    probabilities = np.empty(values.size, dtype=float)
    for position, value in enumerate(values):
        row = baseline.copy()
        row[index] = value
        probabilities[position] = model.predict_proba(row)[0, 1]
    return probabilities


def main() -> int:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(RANDOM_SEED)
    features, labels = _generate_dataset(rng)
    n_samples = features.shape[0]
    n_train = int(round(n_samples * TRAIN_FRACTION))
    permutation = rng.permutation(n_samples)
    train_idx = permutation[:n_train]
    val_idx = permutation[n_train:]
    X_train, y_train = features[train_idx], labels[train_idx]
    X_val, y_val = features[val_idx], labels[val_idx]

    model = train_contrail_model(X_train, y_train, list(FEATURE_NAMES))
    calibrated = fit_calibrator(model, X_val, y_val)

    valid_val = ~np.isnan(y_val)
    X_val_valid = X_val[valid_val]
    y_val_valid = y_val[valid_val]
    raw_probs = model.predict_proba(X_val_valid)[:, 1]
    calibrated_probs = calibrated.predict_proba(X_val_valid)[:, 1]
    raw_brier = compute_brier_score(y_val_valid, raw_probs)
    calibrated_brier = compute_brier_score(y_val_valid, calibrated_probs)

    baseline = np.nanmedian(X_train[~np.isnan(y_train)], axis=0)
    rhi_probs = _sweep(calibrated, baseline, RHI_INDEX, np.linspace(0.1, 1.5, 50))
    margin_probs = _sweep(
        calibrated,
        baseline,
        TLM_K_INDEX,
        np.linspace(baseline[TLM_K_INDEX] - 10.0, baseline[TLM_K_INDEX] + 10.0, 50),
    )
    rhi_monotone = bool(np.all(np.diff(rhi_probs) >= -1e-9))
    margin_monotone = bool(np.all(np.diff(margin_probs) <= 1e-9))

    cold_humid = baseline.copy()
    cold_humid[RHI_INDEX] = 1.25
    cold_humid[TLM_K_INDEX] = baseline[TLM_K_INDEX] - 6.0
    warm_dry = baseline.copy()
    warm_dry[RHI_INDEX] = 0.35
    warm_dry[TLM_K_INDEX] = baseline[TLM_K_INDEX] + 6.0

    train_masked = int(np.isnan(y_train).sum())
    val_masked = int(np.isnan(y_val).sum())
    print("STRATUM Step 5 - Physics-Constrained Correction Model demo")
    print("=" * 64)
    print(f"Synthetic segments: {n_samples} (seed={RANDOM_SEED})")
    print(f"Feature schema: {len(FEATURE_NAMES)} features (frozen Section 12.2 order)")
    print(
        f"Train split:      {y_train.size} rows -> "
        f"{y_train.size - train_masked} labeled, "
        f"{train_masked} NaN-masked (unobservable)"
    )
    print(
        f"Validation split: {y_val.size} rows -> "
        f"{y_val.size - val_masked} labeled, "
        f"{val_masked} NaN-masked (unobservable)"
    )
    print()
    print(f"Raw validation Brier score:          {raw_brier:.5f}")
    print(f"Calibrated validation Brier score:   {calibrated_brier:.5f}")
    print(f"Brier improvement after calibration: {raw_brier - calibrated_brier:+.5f}")
    print()
    rhi_status = "PASS" if rhi_monotone else "FAIL"
    margin_status = "PASS" if margin_monotone else "FAIL"
    print(f"Monotonicity (RHi increasing -> P(persist) non-decreasing):            {rhi_status}")
    print(f"Monotonicity (tlm_k increasing -> P(persist) non-increasing):          {margin_status}")
    print()
    print("Sample predictions (all other features held at the training median):")
    for name, row in (("cold/humid", cold_humid), ("warm/dry", warm_dry)):
        raw_p = float(calibrated.predict_raw_proba(row)[0])
        calibrated_p = float(calibrated.predict_proba(row)[0, 1])
        print(
            f"  {name:>10}: rhi={row[RHI_INDEX]:.2f}, "
            f"tlm_k={row[TLM_K_INDEX]:.1f} K "
            f"-> raw {raw_p:.3f}, calibrated {calibrated_p:.3f}"
        )
    return 0 if (rhi_monotone and margin_monotone) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## scripts/export_codebase.py

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Export the entire STRATUM codebase into COMPLETE_CODEBASE.md.

The exporter walks the source directories ``stratum/``, ``scripts/`` and
``tests/``, collects every Python file, the repository configuration
files, and generated report templates, and compiles them into a single
Markdown reference at the repository root.

The output starts with a tree-style directory overview, a current pytest
status summary (the suite is executed by this script), and per-file
line counts, followed by every collected file as a ``## <path>`` section
inside a syntax-highlighted code fence.

Run from anywhere with the project interpreter::

    .venv/bin/python scripts/export_codebase.py

Binary caches (``__pycache__``, ``*.pyc``), virtual environments, VCS
directories, and large data files (``*.nc``, ``*.parquet``, ...) are
excluded. Files larger than :data:`MAX_FILE_BYTES` are skipped as data.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

#: Repository root: this file lives in ``<root>/scripts/``.
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

#: Name of the generated reference file at the repository root.
OUTPUT_NAME = "COMPLETE_CODEBASE.md"

#: Directories scanned recursively for source files.
SOURCE_DIRS: tuple[str, ...] = ("stratum", "scripts", "tests")

#: Directory names skipped anywhere in the tree.
EXCLUDED_DIR_NAMES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        ".venv",
        "venv",
        "__pycache__",
        ".git",
        ".pytest_cache",
        ".mypy_cache",
        ".ruff_cache",
        ".ipynb_checkpoints",
    }
)

#: File suffixes skipped: binary caches, compiled artifacts, data files.
EXCLUDED_FILE_SUFFIXES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        ".pyc",
        ".pyo",
        ".so",
        ".dylib",
        ".dll",
        ".pyd",
        ".npy",
        ".npz",
        ".joblib",
        ".pkl",
        ".nc",
        ".parquet",
        ".grib",
        ".grib2",
        ".h5",
        ".hdf5",
        ".zip",
        ".gz",
        ".tar",
        ".log",
    }
)

#: Configuration file names picked up from the repository root.
CONFIG_FILE_NAMES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "pyproject.toml",
        "setup.py",
        "setup.cfg",
        "pytest.ini",
        "tox.ini",
        "mypy.ini",
        "requirements.txt",
    }
)

#: Glob patterns for additional root-level configuration files.
CONFIG_GLOBS: tuple[str, ...] = ("requirements-*.txt", "*.toml", "*.cfg", "*.ini")

#: Glob patterns for report templates/artifacts collected from the root.
REPORT_GLOBS: tuple[str, ...] = ("*.html",)

#: Files above this size are treated as data and skipped.
MAX_FILE_BYTES = 1_000_000

#: Markdown fence language per file suffix.
LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX: dict[str, str] = {
    ".py": "python",
    ".toml": "toml",
    ".cfg": "ini",
    ".ini": "ini",
    ".txt": "text",
    ".json": "json",
    ".yml": "yaml",
    ".yaml": "yaml",
    ".html": "html",
    ".md": "markdown",
    ".sh": "bash",
}


def _is_excluded(path: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True when ``path`` is a cache, virtualenv, or data file."""
    if any(part in EXCLUDED_DIR_NAMES for part in path.parts):
        return True
    if path.suffix.lower() in EXCLUDED_FILE_SUFFIXES:
        return True
    return path.name == OUTPUT_NAME


def _collect_source_files() -> list[Path]:
    """Collect all Python files under the configured source directories."""
    collected: list[Path] = []
    for directory in SOURCE_DIRS:
        base = PROJECT_ROOT / directory
        if not base.is_dir():
            continue
        for candidate in base.rglob("*.py"):
            if not _is_excluded(candidate):
                collected.append(candidate)
    return collected


def _collect_root_files() -> list[Path]:
    """Collect root-level Python, configuration, and report files."""
    collected: list[Path] = []
    for candidate in PROJECT_ROOT.iterdir():
        if not candidate.is_file() or _is_excluded(candidate):
            continue
        name = candidate.name
        is_python = candidate.suffix == ".py"
        is_config = name in CONFIG_FILE_NAMES or any(
            candidate.match(pattern) for pattern in CONFIG_GLOBS
        )
        is_report = any(candidate.match(pattern) for pattern in REPORT_GLOBS)
        if is_python or is_config or is_report:
            collected.append(candidate)
    return collected


def _collect_files() -> list[Path]:
    """Collect every file that belongs in the reference, sorted by path."""
    files = {path.resolve() for path in _collect_source_files()}
    files.update(path.resolve() for path in _collect_root_files())
    return sorted(files, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix())


def _run_pytest() -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    """Run the test suite and return ``(exit_code, output_tail_lines)``."""
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "tests/", "-q", "--tb=no"],
        cwd=PROJECT_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    lines = [line.rstrip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    if not lines:
        lines = [line.rstrip() for line in result.stderr.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    return result.returncode, lines


def _summarize_pytest(lines: list[str]) -> str:
    """Extract the one-line pass/fail summary from the pytest tail."""
    for line in reversed(lines):
        lowered = line.lower()
        if "passed" in lowered or "failed" in lowered or "error" in lowered:
            return line.strip()
    return "no pytest summary available"


def _count_lines(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Return ``(total_lines, code_lines)`` for a text file.

    Code lines are non-blank lines whose first non-space character is not
    ``#``; docstring lines count as code.
    """
    try:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except OSError:
        return 0, 0
    total = 0
    code = 0
    for line in text.splitlines():
        total += 1
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped and not stripped.startswith("#"):
            code += 1
    return total, code


def _build_tree(relative_paths: list[str]) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Build a nested dictionary describing the collected file tree."""
    tree: dict[str, object] = {}
    for raw in relative_paths:
        node = tree
        parts = Path(raw).parts
        for part in parts[:-1]:
            node = node.setdefault(part, {})  # type: ignore[assignment]
        node.setdefault("__files__", []).append(parts[-1])  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    return tree


def _render_tree(node: dict[str, object], prefix: str = "") -> list[str]:
    """Render the tree dictionary with box-drawing connectors."""
    lines: list[str] = []
    directories = sorted(key for key in node if key != "__files__")
    files = sorted(node.get("__files__", []))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    entries = [(name, "dir") for name in directories] + [
        (name, "file") for name in files
    ]
    for position, (name, kind) in enumerate(entries):
        last = position == len(entries) - 1
        connector = "└── " if last else "├── "
        suffix = "/" if kind == "dir" else ""
        lines.append(f"{prefix}{connector}{name}{suffix}")
        if kind == "dir":
            extension = "    " if last else "│   "
            lines.extend(_render_tree(node[name], prefix + extension))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    return lines


def _fence_for(content: str, language: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Return opening and closing fences that cannot collide with content."""
    fence = "```"
    while fence in content:
        fence += "`"
    return f"{fence}{language}", fence


def _file_group(relative_path: str) -> str:
    """Group label for the line-count summary."""
    if "/" not in relative_path:
        return "repository root"
    return relative_path.split("/", 1)[0]


def _render_markdown(
    files: list[Path],
    relative_paths: list[str],
    pytest_exit: int,
    pytest_lines: list[str],
    totals: dict[str, tuple[int, int]],
) -> str:
    """Compose the complete Markdown reference document."""
    now = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC", time.gmtime())
    summary = _summarize_pytest(pytest_lines)

    parts: list[str] = []
    parts.append("# Project STRATUM — Complete Codebase Reference\n")
    parts.append(
        f"Generated by `scripts/export_codebase.py` on {now}. "
        f"{len(files)} files exported. Data files (`*.nc`, `*.parquet`), "
        "binary caches, and virtual environments are excluded.\n"
    )

    parts.append("## Directory overview\n")
    parts.append("```text")
    parts.append("stratum-codebase/")
    tree = _build_tree(relative_paths)
    parts.extend(_render_tree(tree))
    parts.append("```\n")

    parts.append("## Test status\n")
    parts.append(
        f"`{sys.executable} -m pytest tests/ -q --tb=no` → **{summary}** "
        f"(exit code {pytest_exit}).\n"
    )
    parts.append("```text")
    parts.extend(pytest_lines[-5:] if pytest_lines else ["no pytest output"])
    parts.append("```\n")

    parts.append("## Line counts\n")
    parts.append("| File | Lines | Code |")
    parts.append("| ---- | ----: | ---: |")
    for relative in relative_paths:
        total, code = totals[relative]
        parts.append(f"| `{relative}` | {total} | {code} |")
    grand_total = sum(total for total, _ in totals.values())
    grand_code = sum(code for _, code in totals.values())
    parts.append(f"| **Total** | **{grand_total}** | **{grand_code}** |")
    parts.append("")

    parts.append("| Group | Files | Lines | Code |")
    parts.append("| ----- | ----: | ----: | ---: |")
    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for relative in relative_paths:
        groups.setdefault(_file_group(relative), []).append(relative)
    for group in sorted(groups):
        members = groups[group]
        lines_total = sum(totals[member][0] for member in members)
        code_total = sum(totals[member][1] for member in members)
        parts.append(
            f"| {group} | {len(members)} | {lines_total} | {code_total} |"
        )
    parts.append("")

    parts.append("## File contents\n")
    for path, relative in zip(files, relative_paths):
        content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if content and not content.endswith("\n"):
            content += "\n"
        language = LANGUAGE_BY_SUFFIX.get(path.suffix.lower(), "text")
        opening, closing = _fence_for(content, language)
        parts.append(f"## {relative}\n")
        parts.append(opening)
        parts.append(content.rstrip("\n"))
        parts.append(closing)
        parts.append("")

    return "\n".join(parts) + "\n"


def main() -> int:
    """Export the codebase and write ``COMPLETE_CODEBASE.md``."""
    files = _collect_files()
    relative_paths = [
        path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT).as_posix() for path in files
    ]

    skipped: list[str] = []
    kept_files: list[Path] = []
    kept_relatives: list[str] = []
    for path, relative in zip(files, relative_paths):
        if path.stat().st_size > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
            skipped.append(relative)
            continue
        kept_files.append(path)
        kept_relatives.append(relative)

    totals = {
        relative: _count_lines(path)
        for path, relative in zip(kept_files, kept_relatives)
    }

    print(f"Exporting {len(kept_files)} files into {OUTPUT_NAME} ...")
    pytest_exit, pytest_lines = _run_pytest()
    print(f"pytest exit code {pytest_exit}: {_summarize_pytest(pytest_lines)}")
    for relative in skipped:
        print(f"  skipped (size > {MAX_FILE_BYTES} bytes): {relative}")

    document = _render_markdown(
        kept_files, kept_relatives, pytest_exit, pytest_lines, totals
    )
    output_path = PROJECT_ROOT / OUTPUT_NAME
    output_path.write_text(document, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Report written: {output_path}")
    print(f"  files: {len(kept_files)} | size: {len(document)} chars")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
````

## scripts/generate_friend_report.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Generate a self-contained HTML progress report for Project STRATUM.

Reuses the Step 8 corridor runner: loads the real ERA5 analysis file
``era5_del_bom_20250101.nc``, synthesizes the 25-waypoint VIDP -> VABB
FL350 flight profile, trains the calibrated LightGBM baseline, evaluates
the corridor with :func:`stratum.pipeline.runner.evaluate_corridor_flight`,
and renders the results as a standalone HTML document
(``stratum_model_progress.html``) with status badges, KPI cards, the ATC
dispatcher advisory, and the full segment-by-segment corridor table.

Exit code is 0 when the report is written.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import importlib.util
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.pipeline.runner import evaluate_corridor_flight  # noqa: E402
from stratum.routes.corridor import AIRPORTS, great_circle_waypoints  # noqa: E402

HTML_FILENAME = "stratum_model_progress.html"
PASSING_TESTS = 120
PERSISTENT_THRESHOLD = 0.50


def _load_flight_script() -> Any:
    """Import ``scripts/run_flight_corridor.py`` as a module."""
    script_path = Path(__file__).resolve().with_name("run_flight_corridor.py")
    module_name = "stratum_flight_corridor"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(module_name, script_path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot import {script_path}")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[module_name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _status_badge(segment: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Map a segment to its contrail status badge (label, CSS class)."""
    if segment["sac_forms"] and segment["p_persist"] >= PERSISTENT_THRESHOLD:
        return "Persistent", "badge-persistent"
    if segment["sac_forms"]:
        return "Aerodynamic", "badge-aerodynamic"
    return "Clear", "badge-clear"


def _decision_text(segment: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Human-readable optimization decision cell text."""
    status = segment["decision_status"]
    if status == "eligible":
        return f"Eligible &#8594; FL{segment['recommended_fl']:.0f}"
    if status == "no_robust_candidate":
        return "No robust candidate"
    if status == "abstain":
        return "Abstain (below threshold)"
    return "&#8212;"


def _render_html(
    *,
    origin: str,
    destination: str,
    departure: pd.Timestamp,
    ist_departure: pd.Timestamp,
    cruise_fl: float,
    num_waypoints: int,
    source_label: str,
    scan_mean_rhi: float,
    scan_hours: int,
    metrics: dict[str, Any],
    result: dict[str, Any],
    aircraft_type: str,
    callsign: str,
    icao24: str,
) -> str:
    """Render the standalone HTML report."""
    origin_lat, origin_lon = AIRPORTS[origin]
    destination_lat, destination_lon = AIRPORTS[destination]
    brier_raw = float(metrics["brier_raw"])
    brier_calibrated = float(metrics["brier_calibrated"])
    brier_improvement = 100.0 * (brier_raw - brier_calibrated) / brier_raw
    reroutes = result["recommended_reroutes"]
    generated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

    if reroutes:
        primary = reroutes[0]
        advisory_body = f"""
        <div class="advisory-grid">
          <div class="advisory-item">
            <div class="advisory-label">Recommended level</div>
            <div class="advisory-value">FL{primary['recommended_fl']:.0f}</div>
            <div class="advisory-sub">{html.escape(primary['direction'])} from FL{primary['baseline_fl']:.0f}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="advisory-item">
            <div class="advisory-label">Fuel penalty</div>
            <div class="advisory-value">+{primary['delta_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg</div>
            <div class="advisory-sub">+{primary['delta_co2_kg']:.2f} kg CO&#8322;</div>
          </div>
          <div class="advisory-item">
            <div class="advisory-label">Avoided warming</div>
            <div class="advisory-value">{primary['avoided_contrail_ef_j'] / 1.0e9:.2f} GJ</div>
            <div class="advisory-sub">benefit/cost {primary['benefit_to_cost_ratio']:.2e}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p class="advisory-note">
          Activated at segment #{primary['segment_index'] + 1}
          ({pd.Timestamp(primary['time_utc']).strftime('%H:%M')} UTC) with
          calibrated P(persist) = {primary['p_persist']:.2f}; covers
          {primary['segments_in_event']} contiguous flagged segment(s).
          Total across {len(reroutes)} event(s): +{result['total_extra_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg fuel,
          +{result['total_extra_co2_kg']:.2f} kg CO&#8322;,
          {result['total_avoidable_warming_j'] / 1.0e9:.2f} GJ avoided warming.
        </p>
        """
    else:
        advisory_body = (
            '<p class="advisory-note">No advisories: calibrated persistence '
            "risk stayed below the 0.40 activation threshold on all segments. "
            "Flying the filed profile costs nothing extra.</p>"
        )

    rows: list[str] = []
    for segment in result["segments"]:
        label, badge_class = _status_badge(segment)
        rows.append(
            f"""
            <tr>
              <td class="num">{segment['segment_index'] + 1}</td>
              <td class="num">{pd.Timestamp(segment['time_utc']).strftime('%H:%M')}</td>
              <td class="num">{segment['lat']:.2f}</td>
              <td class="num">{segment['lon']:.2f}</td>
              <td class="num">{segment['temperature_c']:.1f}</td>
              <td class="num">{segment['rhi'] * 100.0:.0f}%</td>
              <td class="num">{segment['p_persist']:.2f}</td>
              <td><span class="badge {badge_class}">{label}</span></td>
              <td>{_decision_text(segment)}</td>
            </tr>"""
        )
    table_rows = "\n".join(rows)

    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Project STRATUM - Corridor Flight Report</title>
<style>
  :root {{
    --navy: #0f2440;
    --navy-soft: #1c3a5e;
    --ink: #16202e;
    --muted: #5c6b7e;
    --line: #e3e8ef;
    --surface: #f6f8fb;
    --card: #ffffff;
    --green: #0f7a55;
    --green-soft: #e3f4ec;
    --amber: #9a6200;
    --amber-soft: #fdf0d5;
    --red: #b3261e;
    --red-soft: #fbe4e2;
    --blue: #1559a6;
    --blue-soft: #e4eefb;
  }}
  * {{ box-sizing: border-box; }}
  body {{
    margin: 0;
    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto,
      "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
    color: var(--ink);
    background: var(--surface);
    line-height: 1.5;
  }}
  .page {{ max-width: 1080px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 32px 24px 56px; }}
  header {{
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--navy) 0%, var(--navy-soft) 100%);
    color: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 14px;
    padding: 28px 32px;
    margin-bottom: 24px;
  }}
  header h1 {{ margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 26px; letter-spacing: 0.2px; }}
  header .subtitle {{ margin: 0 0 18px; color: #c8d6e8; font-size: 14px; }}
  .badges {{ display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }}
  .pill {{
    display: inline-block;
    padding: 4px 11px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: 600;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28);
    color: #f2f7fd;
  }}
  .pill.ok {{ background: rgba(52, 199, 138, 0.22); border-color: rgba(52, 199, 138, 0.5); }}
  section {{ margin-bottom: 28px; }}
  h2 {{
    font-size: 15px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    color: var(--muted);
    margin: 0 0 12px;
  }}
  .meta-grid {{
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr));
    gap: 10px 24px;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 18px 22px;
    font-size: 14px;
  }}
  .meta-grid dt {{ color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; margin: 0; }}
  .meta-grid dd {{ margin: 2px 0 8px; font-weight: 600; }}
  .kpis {{ display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); gap: 14px; }}
  .kpi {{
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 18px 20px;
  }}
  .kpi .kpi-label {{ font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; color: var(--muted); }}
  .kpi .kpi-value {{ font-size: 26px; font-weight: 700; margin: 6px 0 4px; }}
  .kpi .kpi-sub {{ font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }}
  .kpi .kpi-value.ok {{ color: var(--green); }}
  .advisory {{
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--navy) 0%, #234b7a 100%);
    color: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 14px;
    padding: 24px 28px;
  }}
  .advisory h3 {{ margin: 0 0 14px; font-size: 17px; letter-spacing: 0.3px; }}
  .advisory-grid {{ display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }}
  .advisory-label {{ font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; color: #b9cbe2; }}
  .advisory-value {{ font-size: 28px; font-weight: 700; margin: 4px 0 2px; }}
  .advisory-sub {{ font-size: 13px; color: #c8d6e8; }}
  .advisory-note {{ margin: 16px 0 0; font-size: 13px; color: #d7e3f2; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18); padding-top: 12px; }}
  table {{
    width: 100%;
    border-collapse: collapse;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    overflow: hidden;
    font-size: 13.5px;
  }}
  thead th {{
    text-align: left;
    background: #eef2f8;
    color: #3d4d61;
    font-size: 12px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    padding: 10px 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  }}
  tbody td {{ padding: 8px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eef1f6; }}
  tbody tr:nth-child(even) {{ background: #fafcfe; }}
  tbody tr:last-child td {{ border-bottom: none; }}
  td.num {{ font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }}
  .badge {{ display: inline-block; padding: 2px 10px; border-radius: 999px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; }}
  .badge-clear {{ background: var(--green-soft); color: var(--green); }}
  .badge-aerodynamic {{ background: var(--amber-soft); color: var(--amber); }}
  .badge-persistent {{ background: var(--red-soft); color: var(--red); }}
  footer {{ margin-top: 32px; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--muted); border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 14px; }}
  code {{ background: #eef2f8; padding: 1px 6px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12.5px; }}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="page">

  <header>
    <h1>Project STRATUM</h1>
    <p class="subtitle">Contrail avoidance for Indian air corridors &middot; Step 8 end-to-end corridor flight report</p>
    <div class="badges">
      <span class="pill ok">{PASSING_TESTS} passing tests</span>
      <span class="pill">Real ECMWF ERA5 4D weather</span>
      <span class="pill">{html.escape(origin)} &#8594; {html.escape(destination)} &middot; FL{cruise_fl:.0f}</span>
      <span class="pill">{html.escape(aircraft_type)} &middot; {html.escape(callsign)}</span>
    </div>
  </header>

  <section>
    <h2>Flight metadata</h2>
    <dl class="meta-grid">
      <div><dt>Aircraft</dt><dd>{html.escape(callsign)} (icao24 {html.escape(icao24)}), {html.escape(aircraft_type)}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Route</dt><dd>{html.escape(origin)} ({origin_lat:.2f}N, {origin_lon:.2f}E) &#8594; {html.escape(destination)} ({destination_lat:.2f}N, {destination_lon:.2f}E)</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Profile</dt><dd>{num_waypoints} cruise waypoints, FL{cruise_fl:.0f}, 6 min spacing</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Departure</dt><dd>{departure.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')} UTC ({ist_departure.strftime('%H:%M')} IST)</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Weather data</dt><dd>{html.escape(source_label)}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>Departure scan</dt><dd>Worst-case hour at FL{cruise_fl:.0f}: route-mean RHi {scan_mean_rhi:.2f} over {scan_hours} candidate hours</dd></div>
    </dl>
  </section>

  <section>
    <h2>Key indicators</h2>
    <div class="kpis">
      <div class="kpi">
        <div class="kpi-label">Model status</div>
        <div class="kpi-value ok">Trained &amp; calibrated</div>
        <div class="kpi-sub">LightGBM (monotone constraints) + isotonic regression &middot; {metrics['n_train']} train / {metrics['n_val']} val rows &middot; {metrics['n_masked']} NaN-masked labels</div>
      </div>
      <div class="kpi">
        <div class="kpi-label">Calibration (val Brier)</div>
        <div class="kpi-value">{brier_calibrated:.4f}</div>
        <div class="kpi-sub">raw {brier_raw:.4f} &#8594; calibrated {brier_calibrated:.4f} ({brier_improvement:.1f}% improvement)</div>
      </div>
      <div class="kpi">
        <div class="kpi-label">Persistent segments</div>
        <div class="kpi-value">{result['contrail_persistent_segments']} / {result['total_segments']}</div>
        <div class="kpi-sub">{result['contrail_forming_segments']} SAC-forming &middot; {result['flagged_segments']} flagged for avoidance</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section>
    <h2>ATC dispatcher advisory</h2>
    <div class="advisory">
      <h3>Contrail avoidance action</h3>
      {advisory_body}
    </div>
  </section>

  <section>
    <h2>Corridor segments (25 waypoints, VIDP &#8594; VABB)</h2>
    <table>
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>#</th><th>UTC</th><th>Lat</th><th>Lon</th><th>Temp &deg;C</th>
          <th>RHi</th><th>P(persist)</th><th>Contrail status</th><th>Decision</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {table_rows}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </section>

  <footer>
    Generated by <code>scripts/generate_friend_report.py</code> on {generated_at}.
    Data source: real ERA5 analysis, interpolated along the great-circle VIDP&#8594;VABB profile.
    Contrail status: Clear (no SAC formation), Aerodynamic (forms, predicted short-lived),
    Persistent (calibrated P(persist) &#8805; 0.50).
  </footer>

</div>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main() -> int:
    """Run the corridor evaluation and write the HTML report."""
    flight_script = _load_flight_script()
    ds_met, source_label = flight_script._load_met()
    origin, destination = flight_script.ROUTE
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS[origin],
        AIRPORTS[destination],
        num_points=flight_script.N_WAYPOINTS,
        fl=flight_script.CRUISE_FL,
    )
    departure, scan_mean_rhi, scan_hours = flight_script._select_departure_time(
        ds_met, waypoints, flight_script.CRUISE_FL
    )
    flight = flight_script._synthesize_flight(departure)
    model, metrics = flight_script._train_baseline()
    result = evaluate_corridor_flight(
        flight, ds_met, model, aircraft_type=flight_script.AIRCRAFT_TYPE
    )

    report_html = _render_html(
        origin=origin,
        destination=destination,
        departure=departure,
        ist_departure=departure + pd.Timedelta(minutes=flight_script.IST_OFFSET_MINUTES),
        cruise_fl=flight_script.CRUISE_FL,
        num_waypoints=flight_script.N_WAYPOINTS,
        source_label=source_label,
        scan_mean_rhi=scan_mean_rhi,
        scan_hours=scan_hours,
        metrics=metrics,
        result=result,
        aircraft_type=flight_script.AIRCRAFT_TYPE,
        callsign=flight_script.CALLSIGN,
        icao24=flight_script.ICAO24,
    )

    output_path = PROJECT_ROOT / HTML_FILENAME
    output_path.write_text(report_html, encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"Report written: {output_path}")
    print(
        f"Corridor: {result['total_segments']} segments | "
        f"{result['contrail_forming_segments']} SAC-forming | "
        f"{result['contrail_persistent_segments']} persistent | "
        f"{len(result['recommended_reroutes'])} advisory event(s) | "
        f"val Brier {metrics['brier_raw']:.4f} -> {metrics['brier_calibrated']:.4f}"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## scripts/run_flight_corridor.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run the STRATUM end-to-end pipeline on a Delhi-Mumbai corridor flight.

Synthesizes a realistic VIDP -> VABB cruise profile with 25 waypoints,
loads the real ERA5 analysis file ``era5_del_bom_20250101.nc`` (falling
back to an in-memory synthetic dataset with download guidance), trains a
calibrated LightGBM baseline, runs
:func:`stratum.pipeline.evaluate_corridor_flight`, and prints an executive
flight report: flight metadata, segment-by-segment atmospheric and
contrail risk, and an ATC dispatcher advisory with recommended altitude
deviations, fuel deltas and avoided climate impact.

Exit code is 0 when the report completes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
import warnings
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import xarray as xr

# The netCDF4 1.7.2 wheel can emit a spurious NumPy binary-compatibility
# RuntimeWarning on first engine use; functionality is exercised below.
warnings.filterwarnings(
    "ignore",
    message="numpy.ndarray size changed, may indicate binary incompatibility",
    category=RuntimeWarning,
)

# LightGBM's sklearn wrapper validates feature names even when predictions
# receive plain ndarrays fitted from ndarrays; silence its spurious warning.
warnings.filterwarnings(
    "ignore",
    message="X does not have valid feature names, "
    "but LGBMClassifier was fitted with feature names",
    category=UserWarning,
)

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(PROJECT_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))

from stratum.io.era5 import (  # noqa: E402
    interpolate_met_to_segment,
    load_era5_dataset,
)
from stratum.model.calibrate import CalibratedModel, fit_calibrator  # noqa: E402
from stratum.model.features import (  # noqa: E402
    FEATURE_NAMES,
    extract_features_row,
    feature_vector,
)
from stratum.model.train import compute_brier_score, train_contrail_model  # noqa: E402
from stratum.physics import e_i, fl_to_p, get_aircraft_params, solve_TLM  # noqa: E402
from stratum.pipeline import evaluate_corridor_flight  # noqa: E402
from stratum.routes.corridor import AIRPORTS, great_circle_waypoints  # noqa: E402

ERA5_FILENAME = "era5_del_bom_20250101.nc"
CALLSIGN = "AIC866"
ICAO24 = "800abc"
ROUTE = ("VIDP", "VABB")
AIRCRAFT_TYPE = "A320"
CRUISE_FL = 350.0
N_WAYPOINTS = 25
STEP_MINUTES = 6
TRAIN_SAMPLES = 3000
RNG_SEED = 42
IST_OFFSET_MINUTES = 330
RHI_CENTRE = 0.90
MARGIN_CENTRE = -4.0
RHI_SLOPE = 3.0
MARGIN_SLOPE = 0.15
RICHARDSON_CENTRE = 1.0
RICHARDSON_SLOPE = 0.5
ETA_CENTRE = 0.32
ETA_SLOPE = 4.0
LABEL_FLIP_PROBABILITY = 0.03
MASKED_LABEL_FRACTION = 0.25
TRAIN_FRACTION = 0.65
EPS = 0.622
GJ_IN_J = 1.0e9


def _candidate_paths() -> list[Path]:
    """Locations searched for the real ERA5 file, in order."""
    return [
        PROJECT_ROOT / ERA5_FILENAME,
        PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / ERA5_FILENAME,
        Path.cwd() / ERA5_FILENAME,
    ]


def _find_era5_file() -> Path | None:
    """Return the first existing candidate path, or ``None``."""
    for candidate in _candidate_paths():
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
    return None


def _build_mock_era5() -> xr.Dataset:
    """Fallback synthetic dataset when the real ERA5 file is absent.

    Mid-route humidity is tuned so the corridor contains an
    ice-supersaturated pocket, which keeps the fallback run meaningful.
    """
    levels = np.array([200.0, 225.0, 250.0, 300.0, 350.0, 400.0])
    latitudes = np.linspace(15.0, 32.0, 10)
    longitudes = np.linspace(68.0, 82.0, 10)
    times = np.arange(
        "2025-01-01T00:00", "2025-01-01T09:00", dtype="datetime64[h]"
    ).astype("datetime64[ns]")
    shape = (levels.size, latitudes.size, longitudes.size, times.size)

    level = levels[:, None, None, None]
    latitude = latitudes[None, :, None, None]
    longitude = longitudes[None, None, :, None]

    temperature = 216.65 + 0.11 * (level - 200.0)
    moisture_pocket = np.exp(-((longitude - 74.5) ** 2) / 8.0) * np.exp(
        -((level - 250.0) ** 2) / 4000.0
    )
    target_rhi = 0.35 + 1.15 * moisture_pocket
    saturation = np.array(
        [e_i(216.65 + 0.11 * (value - 200.0)) for value in levels]
    )[:, None, None, None]
    specific_humidity = target_rhi * saturation * EPS / (level * 100.0)

    u_wind = 8.0 + 0.05 * (latitude - 15.0) + 0.004 * (level - 200.0)
    v_wind = -3.0 + 0.001 * (level - 200.0)

    dataset = xr.Dataset(
        data_vars={
            "temperature": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(temperature, shape),
            ),
            "specific_humidity": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(specific_humidity, shape),
            ),
            "u_component_of_wind": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(u_wind, shape),
            ),
            "v_component_of_wind": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(v_wind, shape),
            ),
            "vertical_velocity": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.full(shape, 0.002),
            ),
            "fraction_of_cloud_cover": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.full(shape, 0.25),
            ),
        },
        coords={
            "level": levels,
            "latitude": latitudes,
            "longitude": longitudes,
            "time": times,
        },
    )
    return dataset.transpose("time", "level", "latitude", "longitude")


def _load_met() -> tuple[xr.Dataset, str]:
    """Load the real ERA5 file, or the synthetic fallback with a note."""
    path = _find_era5_file()
    if path is not None:
        return load_era5_dataset(str(path)), f"real ERA5 file {path}"
    return _build_mock_era5(), (
        "synthetic mock dataset - place "
        f"{ERA5_FILENAME} in the project root or data/ to use real analysis"
    )


def _rhi_at(
    ds_met: xr.Dataset, latitude: float, longitude: float, pressure_pa: float, time: Any
) -> float:
    """Ice-relative humidity from an interpolated ERA5 state."""
    met = interpolate_met_to_segment(ds_met, latitude, longitude, pressure_pa, time)
    humidity = met["specific_humidity"]
    vapor_pressure = humidity * pressure_pa / (EPS + (1.0 - EPS) * humidity)
    return vapor_pressure / e_i(met["temperature"])


def _select_departure_time(
    ds_met: xr.Dataset,
    waypoints: list[dict[str, Any]],
    cruise_fl: float,
) -> tuple[pd.Timestamp, float, int]:
    """Pick the worst-case departure hour for the corridor at cruise level.

    Scans every available ERA5 time whose flight window fits inside the
    dataset, probes a subsampled set of waypoints, and returns the hour
    with the highest route-mean RHi (plus that mean and the number of
    scanned candidate hours).
    """
    times = np.asarray(ds_met["time"].values)
    window = np.timedelta64((N_WAYPOINTS - 1) * STEP_MINUTES, "m")
    pressure_pa = fl_to_p(cruise_fl) * 100.0
    probes = waypoints[::3]

    best_time: pd.Timestamp | None = None
    best_mean = float("nan")
    scanned = 0
    for index in range(times.size):
        candidate = times[index]
        if candidate + window > times[-1]:
            break
        scanned += 1
        values = [
            _rhi_at(ds_met, waypoint["lat"], waypoint["lon"], pressure_pa, candidate)
            for waypoint in probes
        ]
        mean_rhi = float(np.nanmean(values))
        if best_time is None or mean_rhi > best_mean:
            best_time = pd.Timestamp(candidate).tz_localize("UTC")
            best_mean = mean_rhi
    if best_time is None:
        fallback = pd.Timestamp(times[0]).tz_localize("UTC")
        return fallback, float("nan"), 0
    return best_time, best_mean, scanned


def _synthesize_flight(departure: pd.Timestamp) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Cleaned ADS-B shaped cruise profile for VIDP -> VABB."""
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS[ROUTE[0]], AIRPORTS[ROUTE[1]], num_points=N_WAYPOINTS, fl=CRUISE_FL
    )
    records = []
    for index, waypoint in enumerate(waypoints):
        records.append(
            {
                "icao24": ICAO24,
                "callsign": CALLSIGN,
                "time": departure + pd.Timedelta(minutes=STEP_MINUTES * index),
                "lat": waypoint["lat"],
                "lon": waypoint["lon"],
                "baro_alt_ft": CRUISE_FL * 100.0,
                "gs_kt": 455.0,
                "track_deg": 214.0,
                "vrate_fpm": 0.0,
                "on_ground": False,
                "source": "opensky",
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(records)


def _train_baseline() -> tuple[CalibratedModel, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Train the calibrated LightGBM baseline on a synthetic risk population.

    The ground-truth logit is monotone in the canonical features (RHi up,
    TLM margin down, Richardson number down, engine efficiency up) and
    the labels carry a small flip probability plus a quarter of
    non-observable NaNs, mirroring the Step 5 protocol.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(RNG_SEED)
    engine_eta = float(get_aircraft_params(AIRCRAFT_TYPE)["eta"])
    vectors: list[np.ndarray] = []
    labels: list[float] = []

    for _ in range(TRAIN_SAMPLES):
        flight_level = float(rng.uniform(300.0, 400.0))
        pressure_pa = fl_to_p(flight_level) * 100.0
        tlm_k = solve_TLM(pressure_pa, engine_eta)
        margin = float(rng.uniform(-12.0, 4.0))
        rhi = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.6))
        rhi_above = max(0.0, rhi + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1)))
        rhi_below = max(0.0, rhi + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1)))
        richardson_number = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 4.0))
        solar_zenith_deg = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 180.0))
        engine_efficiency_eta = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.40))
        log_soot_ei_n = float(rng.uniform(14.0, 16.0))

        row = extract_features_row(
            rhi=rhi,
            rhi_above=rhi_above,
            rhi_below=rhi_below,
            rhi_grad=rhi_above - rhi_below,
            temp_k=tlm_k + margin,
            tlm_k=tlm_k,
            tlc_k=tlm_k - float(rng.uniform(0.5, 8.0)),
            pressure_pa=pressure_pa,
            cloud_ice=float(rng.random() < 0.2),
            high_cloud_fraction=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.8)),
            vertical_velocity_omega=float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05)),
            vertical_wind_shear=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.0e-4)),
            dist_to_tropopause_m=float(rng.uniform(9000.0, 12500.0)) - 16000.0,
            lat=float(rng.uniform(15.0, 32.0)),
            lon=float(rng.uniform(68.0, 82.0)),
            month=1.0,
            local_solar_time_hours=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 24.0)),
            richardson_number=richardson_number,
            solar_zenith_deg=solar_zenith_deg,
            engine_efficiency_eta=engine_efficiency_eta,
            log_soot_ei_n=log_soot_ei_n,
        )
        vectors.append(feature_vector(row))

        logit = (
            RHI_SLOPE * (rhi - RHI_CENTRE)
            - MARGIN_SLOPE * (margin - MARGIN_CENTRE)
            - RICHARDSON_SLOPE * (richardson_number - RICHARDSON_CENTRE)
            + ETA_SLOPE * (engine_efficiency_eta - ETA_CENTRE)
        )
        probability = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-logit))
        label = 1.0 if rng.random() < probability else 0.0
        if rng.random() < LABEL_FLIP_PROBABILITY:
            label = 1.0 - label
        if rng.random() < MASKED_LABEL_FRACTION:
            label = float("nan")
        labels.append(label)

    features = np.vstack(vectors)
    label_array = np.asarray(labels, dtype=float)
    observed = np.isfinite(label_array)
    split = int(TRAIN_FRACTION * TRAIN_SAMPLES)
    indices = np.arange(TRAIN_SAMPLES)
    train_indices = indices[(indices < split) & observed]
    val_indices = indices[(indices >= split) & observed]

    model = train_contrail_model(
        features[train_indices], label_array[train_indices], list(FEATURE_NAMES)
    )
    calibrated = fit_calibrator(
        model, features[val_indices], label_array[val_indices]
    )
    raw = calibrated.predict_raw_proba(features[val_indices])
    calibrated_probability = calibrated.predict_proba(features[val_indices])[:, 1]
    metrics = {
        "n_samples": TRAIN_SAMPLES,
        "n_train": int(train_indices.size),
        "n_val": int(val_indices.size),
        "n_masked": int(np.sum(~observed)),
        "brier_raw": compute_brier_score(label_array[val_indices], raw),
        "brier_calibrated": compute_brier_score(
            label_array[val_indices], calibrated_probability
        ),
    }
    return calibrated, metrics


def _format_time(time_utc: str) -> str:
    """``HH:MM`` UTC from an ISO-8601 timestamp string."""
    return pd.Timestamp(time_utc).strftime("%H:%M")


def _print_report(
    flight: pd.DataFrame,
    departure: pd.Timestamp,
    scan_mean_rhi: float,
    scan_hours: int,
    source_label: str,
    metrics: dict[str, Any],
    result: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    """Print the executive flight report."""
    origin, destination = ROUTE
    origin_lat, origin_lon = AIRPORTS[origin]
    destination_lat, destination_lon = AIRPORTS[destination]
    arrival = departure + pd.Timedelta(minutes=STEP_MINUTES * (N_WAYPOINTS - 1))
    ist_departure = departure + pd.Timedelta(minutes=IST_OFFSET_MINUTES)

    print("=" * 78)
    print("STRATUM Step 8 - End-to-End Corridor Flight Report")
    print("=" * 78)
    print("Flight metadata")
    print("-" * 78)
    print(f"  Aircraft         : {CALLSIGN} (icao24 {ICAO24}), type {AIRCRAFT_TYPE}")
    print(
        f"  Route            : {origin} ({origin_lat:.2f}N, {origin_lon:.2f}E) -> "
        f"{destination} ({destination_lat:.2f}N, {destination_lon:.2f}E)"
    )
    print(
        f"  Profile          : great-circle, {N_WAYPOINTS} cruise waypoints, "
        f"FL{CRUISE_FL:.0f}, {STEP_MINUTES} min spacing"
    )
    print(
        f"  Departure        : {departure.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')} UTC "
        f"({ist_departure.strftime('%H:%M')} IST), arrival "
        f"{arrival.strftime('%H:%M')} UTC"
    )
    print(f"  Trajectory rows  : {len(flight)}")
    print(f"  Weather data     : {source_label}")
    if scan_hours > 0:
        print(
            f"  Departure scan   : worst-case hour for FL{CRUISE_FL:.0f} "
            f"(route-mean RHi {scan_mean_rhi:.3f} over {scan_hours} candidate "
            "hours)"
        )
    print(
        f"  Model baseline   : LightGBM monotone + isotonic | "
        f"{metrics['n_train']} train / {metrics['n_val']} val rows "
        f"({metrics['n_masked']} NaN-masked) | val Brier "
        f"{metrics['brier_raw']:.4f} -> {metrics['brier_calibrated']:.4f}"
    )

    print()
    print("Segment-by-segment atmospheric and contrail risk breakdown")
    print("-" * 78)
    header = (
        f"{'#':>3}  {'UTC':>5}  {'lat':>7}  {'lon':>7}  {'FL':>3}  "
        f"{'T[C]':>6}  {'RHi':>5}  {'P(pers)':>7}  {'SAC':>4}  "
        f"{'ice':>3}  decision"
    )
    print(header)
    for segment in result["segments"]:
        temperature = segment["temperature_c"]
        rhi = segment["rhi"]
        decision = segment["decision_status"] or "-"
        if segment["decision_status"] == "eligible":
            decision = f"eligible -> FL{segment['recommended_fl']:.0f}"
        elif segment["decision_status"] == "no_robust_candidate":
            decision = "no robust candidate"
        print(
            f"{segment['segment_index'] + 1:>3}  "
            f"{_format_time(segment['time_utc']):>5}  "
            f"{segment['lat']:>7.2f}  {segment['lon']:>7.2f}  "
            f"{segment['flight_level']:>3.0f}  "
            f"{temperature:>6.1f}  {rhi:>5.2f}  "
            f"{segment['p_persist']:>7.3f}  "
            f"{'yes' if segment['sac_forms'] else 'no':>4}  "
            f"{'yes' if segment['ambient_ice_supersaturated'] else 'no':>3}  "
            f"{decision}"
        )

    print()
    print("ATC Dispatcher Advisory")
    print("-" * 78)
    reroutes = result["recommended_reroutes"]
    if not reroutes:
        print(
            "  No advisories: calibrated persistence risk stayed below the "
            "0.40 activation threshold (or no robust candidate) on all "
            "segments; flying the filed profile costs nothing extra."
        )
    else:
        for reroute in reroutes:
            print(
                f"  Event {reroute['event_index'] + 1}: segment "
                f"#{reroute['segment_index'] + 1} at "
                f"{_format_time(reroute['time_utc'])} UTC, "
                f"FL{reroute['baseline_fl']:.0f} -> "
                f"FL{reroute['recommended_fl']:.0f} ({reroute['direction']})"
            )
            print(
                f"    Calibrated P(persist): {reroute['p_persist']:.3f} | "
                f"{reroute['segments_in_event']} contiguous flagged segment(s)"
            )
            print(
                f"    Delta fuel: +{reroute['delta_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg | "
                f"delta CO2: +{reroute['delta_co2_kg']:.2f} kg"
            )
            print(
                f"    Avoided contrail warming: "
                f"{reroute['avoided_contrail_ef_j'] / GJ_IN_J:.2f} GJ | "
                f"benefit-to-cost ratio "
                f"{reroute['benefit_to_cost_ratio']:.3e}"
            )
        advisories = f"{len(reroutes)} advisory event" + (
            "s" if len(reroutes) != 1 else ""
        )
        print(
            f"  Totals: {advisories} | "
            f"+{result['total_extra_fuel_kg']:.2f} kg fuel | "
            f"+{result['total_extra_co2_kg']:.2f} kg CO2 | "
            f"{result['total_avoidable_warming_j'] / GJ_IN_J:.2f} GJ avoided "
            "warming"
        )

    print()
    print("Corridor summary")
    print("-" * 78)
    print(
        f"  Segments: {result['total_segments']} total | "
        f"{result['contrail_forming_segments']} SAC-forming | "
        f"{result['contrail_persistent_segments']} predicted persistent | "
        f"{result['flagged_segments']} flagged for avoidance"
    )
    print()


def main() -> int:
    """Run the corridor flight report and return the process exit code."""
    ds_met, source_label = _load_met()
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS[ROUTE[0]], AIRPORTS[ROUTE[1]], num_points=N_WAYPOINTS, fl=CRUISE_FL
    )
    departure, scan_mean_rhi, scan_hours = _select_departure_time(
        ds_met, waypoints, CRUISE_FL
    )
    flight = _synthesize_flight(departure)
    model, metrics = _train_baseline()
    result = evaluate_corridor_flight(flight, ds_met, model, aircraft_type=AIRCRAFT_TYPE)
