## stratum/physics/diagnostics.py

```python
"""Atmospheric diagnostics and aircraft parameter lookups."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np

__all__ = [
    "AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE",
    "get_aircraft_params",
    "compute_richardson_number",
    "compute_solar_zenith_angle",
]

# Gas constants and thermal parameters
G = 9.80665              # Gravitational acceleration [m/s^2]
R_D = 287.05             # Specific gas constant for dry air [J/(kg K)]
C_P = 1004.0             # Specific heat capacity at constant pressure [J/(kg K)]
KAPPA = R_D / C_P        # 2/7 ≈ 0.286

# Certified default parameters by aircraft ICAO designator
# nvPM number emissions index (EI_n) [particles / kg fuel] and cruise efficiency (eta)
AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {
    "A320": {"eta": 0.32, "ei_n": 1.5e15, "q_fuel": 43.1e6},
    "A20N": {"eta": 0.38, "ei_n": 4.0e14, "q_fuel": 43.2e6},  # A320neo (LEAP-1A)
    "B738": {"eta": 0.31, "ei_n": 1.8e15, "q_fuel": 43.1e6},  # 737-800 (CFM56-7B)
    "B38M": {"eta": 0.37, "ei_n": 5.0e14, "q_fuel": 43.2e6},  # 737 MAX 8
    "B77W": {"eta": 0.35, "ei_n": 8.0e14, "q_fuel": 43.0e6},  # 777-300ER (GE90)
    "DEFAULT": {"eta": 0.33, "ei_n": 1.0e15, "q_fuel": 43.1e6},
}


def get_aircraft_params(aircraft_type: str) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Retrieve certified propulsion efficiency and soot emission parameters."""
    return AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE.get(
        aircraft_type.upper(), AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE["DEFAULT"]
    )


def compute_richardson_number(
    temp_above_k: float,
    temp_below_k: float,
    u_above: float,
    u_below: float,
    v_above: float,
    v_below: float,
    alt_above_m: float,
    alt_below_m: float,
    p_ambient_pa: float,
) -> float:
    """Calculate the Gradient Richardson Number (Ri) between adjacent flight levels.

    Ri = (g / theta) * (d_theta / dz) / ((du/dz)^2 + (dv/dz)^2)
    Ri < 0.25 indicates dynamic instability/turbulence that shears contrails apart.
    """
    dz = alt_above_m - alt_below_m
    if abs(dz) < 1.0:
        return 1.0  # Stable fallback if layers collapse

    # Potential temperature: theta = T * (100000 / p)^kappa
    theta_above = temp_above_k * (100000.0 / (p_ambient_pa - 2500.0)) ** KAPPA
    theta_below = temp_below_k * (100000.0 / (p_ambient_pa + 2500.0)) ** KAPPA
    theta_mid = 0.5 * (theta_above + theta_below)

    d_theta_dz = (theta_above - theta_below) / dz
    du_dz = (u_above - u_below) / dz
    dv_dz = (v_above - v_below) / dz

    shear_squared = du_dz**2 + dv_dz**2
    if shear_squared < 1e-8:
        return 10.0  # High stability, virtually zero mechanical shear

    ri = (G / theta_mid) * d_theta_dz / shear_squared
    # Clamp Ri to avoid numerical overflow in extreme gradients
    return float(np.clip(ri, -5.0, 10.0))


def compute_solar_zenith_angle(dt_utc: datetime, lat_deg: float, lon_deg: float) -> float:
    """Calculate the solar zenith angle in degrees (0° = directly overhead, >90° = night)."""
    # Day of year and fractional hour
    day_of_year = dt_utc.timetuple().tm_yday
    hour_utc = dt_utc.hour + dt_utc.minute / 60.0 + dt_utc.second / 3600.0

    # Solar declination approximation (radians)
    gamma = 2.0 * math.pi / 365.0 * (day_of_year - 1 + (hour_utc - 12.0) / 24.0)
    declination = (
        0.006918
        - 0.399912 * math.cos(gamma)
        + 0.070257 * math.sin(gamma)
        - 0.006758 * math.cos(2 * gamma)
        + 0.000907 * math.sin(2 * gamma)
    )

    # Equation of time in minutes
    eq_time_min = 229.18 * (
        0.000075
        + 0.001868 * math.cos(gamma)
        - 0.032077 * math.sin(gamma)
        - 0.014615 * math.cos(2 * gamma)
        - 0.040849 * math.sin(2 * gamma)
    )

    # Solar hour angle (radians)
    time_offset = eq_time_min + 4.0 * lon_deg
    solar_time_min = (hour_utc * 60.0 + time_offset) % 1440.0
    hour_angle = math.radians((solar_time_min / 4.0) - 180.0)

    lat_rad = math.radians(lat_deg)
    cos_zenith = math.sin(lat_rad) * math.sin(declination) + math.cos(
        lat_rad
    ) * math.cos(declination) * math.cos(hour_angle)

    zenith_rad = math.acos(np.clip(cos_zenith, -1.0, 1.0))
    return math.degrees(zenith_rad)
```

## stratum/physics/sac.py

```python
"""Schmidt-Appleman criterion (SAC) for contrail formation.

Implements the mixing-line slope ``G``, the liquid saturation threshold
temperature ``T_LM``, the critical ambient temperature ``T_LC``, and a
single-point evaluation of the Schmidt-Appleman criterion for Project
STRATUM.

Engine microphysics enters through three parameters: the propulsion
efficiency ``eta``, the fuel lower heating value ``q_fuel`` [J/kg], and
the water emission index ``ei_h2o`` [kg(H2O)/kg(fuel)]. The generic entry
point :func:`compute_sac_slope` carries the engine-registry defaults
(``eta=0.32``, ``q_fuel=43.1e6``, ``ei_h2o=1.25``). The module constants
:data:`EI_H2O` (1.23) and :data:`Q` (43.2e6) are the Step-1 verification
set; :func:`G`, :func:`solve_TLM` and :func:`evaluate_sac` keep those as
their defaults (with ``eta=0.30``) so the pinned Step-1 quantities are
unchanged. Callers that know the aircraft pass the registry values from
:func:`stratum.physics.diagnostics.get_aircraft_params` explicitly.

References
----------
Schumann, U. (1996). On conditions for contrail formation from aircraft
exhausts. Meteorologische Zeitschrift, 5(1), 4-23.

Ponater, M., Marquart, S., Sausen, R., & Schumann, U. (2002). Contrails
in a comprehensive global climate model. Journal of Geophysical
Research, 107(D13).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from stratum.physics import thermo

__all__ = [
    "EI_H2O",
    "c_p",
    "eps",
    "Q",
    "compute_sac_slope",
    "G",
    "solve_TLM",
    "critical_T",
    "evaluate_sac",
]

#: Emission index of water vapor [kg(H2O)/kg(fuel)].
EI_H2O: float = 1.23
#: Specific heat capacity of ambient air [J/(kg*K)].
c_p: float = 1004.0
#: Ratio of molar masses of water vapor and dry air [-].
eps: float = 0.622
#: Specific combustion heat of the fuel [J/kg].
Q: float = 43.2e6

#: Below this water relative humidity the solution of eq. (11) is used;
#: at or above it the mixing line is tangent at T_LM and T_LC = T_LM.
_RHW_TANGENT_LEVEL: float = 0.999

#: Temperature bracket [K] containing the unique solution of
#: ``d(e_w)/dT = G`` for all physically relevant slopes.
_TLM_BRACKET_K: tuple[float, float] = (180.0, 273.15)
#: Cold bound [K] of the bracket for the T_LC root search.
_TLC_COLD_BOUND_K: float = 160.0
#: Number of bisection halvings (enough to reach double precision).
_BISECTION_ITERATIONS: int = 200

_KELVIN_OFFSET: float = 273.15


def compute_sac_slope(
    pressure_pa: float,
    eta: float = 0.32,
    q_fuel: float = 43.1e6,
    ei_h2o: float = 1.25,
) -> float:
    """Mixing-line slope G [Pa/K] for the given engine parameters.

    ``G = (c_p * p * ei_h2o) / (eps * q_fuel * (1 - eta))`` after
    Schumann (1996), eq. (4). ``eta`` is the propulsion efficiency
    (0-1), ``q_fuel`` the fuel lower heating value [J/kg], and
    ``ei_h2o`` the water emission index [kg(H2O)/kg(fuel)].
    """
    return (c_p * pressure_pa * ei_h2o) / (eps * q_fuel * (1.0 - eta))


def G(
    p_Pa: float,
    eta: float = 0.30,
    q_fuel: float = Q,
    ei_h2o: float = EI_H2O,
) -> float:
    """Mixing-line slope G [Pa/K] at ambient pressure ``p_Pa`` [Pa].

    Legacy entry point kept numerically identical to the Step-1
    verification set: default ``eta=0.30``, ``q_fuel=Q``, and
    ``ei_h2o=EI_H2O``. Engine-registry callers use
    :func:`compute_sac_slope` or pass the aircraft values explicitly.
    Schumann (1996), eq. (4).
    """
    return compute_sac_slope(p_Pa, eta=eta, q_fuel=q_fuel, ei_h2o=ei_h2o)


def solve_TLM(
    p_Pa: float,
    eta: float = 0.30,
    q_fuel: float = Q,
    ei_h2o: float = EI_H2O,
) -> float:
    """Temperature T_LM [K] where the saturation curve has slope G.

    Solves ``d(e_w)/dT(T) = G(p_Pa, eta, q_fuel, ei_h2o)`` by bisection.
    Schumann (1996), eq. (10). T_LM is the warmest temperature at which
    the plume mixing line is saturated (100% RH over liquid). Defaults
    match the Step-1 verification set.
    """
    g = G(p_Pa, eta, q_fuel=q_fuel, ei_h2o=ei_h2o)
    lo, hi = _TLM_BRACKET_K
    if not (thermo.d_e_w_dT(lo) < g < thermo.d_e_w_dT(hi)):
        raise ValueError(
            "G={g:.6f} Pa/K outside the solved bracket for p={p:.1f} Pa".format(
                g=g, p=p_Pa
            )
        )
    for _ in range(_BISECTION_ITERATIONS):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if thermo.d_e_w_dT(mid) < g:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return 0.5 * (lo + hi)


def critical_T(rhw: float, TLM: float, G: float) -> float:
    """Critical ambient temperature T_LC [K] for water RH ``rhw`` [-].

    Solves Schumann (1996), eq. (11)::

        rhw * e_w(T_LC) = e_w(TLM) + G * (T_LC - TLM)

    The Schmidt-Appleman criterion holds for ambient temperatures below
    T_LC. For ``rhw >= 0.999`` the mixing line is tangent to the
    saturation curve at T_LM itself, so T_LM is returned (Schumann 1996,
    p. 10).
    """
    if rhw <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"rhw must be positive, got {rhw!r}")
    if rhw >= _RHW_TANGENT_LEVEL:
        return TLM

    e_w_tlm = thermo.e_w(TLM)

    def residual(t: float) -> float:
        return rhw * thermo.e_w(t) - e_w_tlm - G * (t - TLM)

    # residual > 0 at the cold bound and < 0 at T_LM; the root between the
    # two is the relevant (cold) crossing.
    lo, hi = _TLC_COLD_BOUND_K, TLM
    if residual(lo) <= 0.0 or residual(hi) >= 0.0:
        return TLM
    for _ in range(_BISECTION_ITERATIONS):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if residual(mid) > 0.0:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return 0.5 * (lo + hi)


def evaluate_sac(
    p_Pa: float,
    T_K: float,
    rhi: float,
    eta: float = 0.30,
    q_fuel: float = Q,
    ei_h2o: float = EI_H2O,
) -> dict[str, float | bool]:
    """Evaluate the Schmidt-Appleman criterion at a single point.

    Parameters
    ----------
    p_Pa : float
        Ambient pressure [Pa].
    T_K : float
        Ambient temperature [K].
    rhi : float
        Ambient relative humidity over ice [-]. It is converted to
        relative humidity over water before evaluating the SAC, because
        the criterion is defined against the liquid saturation curve.
    eta : float, optional
        Propulsion efficiency (0-1), by default 0.30 (Step-1 set).
    q_fuel : float, optional
        Fuel lower heating value [J/kg], by default :data:`Q`.
    ei_h2o : float, optional
        Water emission index [kg/kg], by default :data:`EI_H2O`.

    Returns
    -------
    dict
        Mapping with keys:

        - ``forms``: bool, True if the SAC holds (contrail forms).
        - ``persists``: bool, True if the ambient air is
          ice-supersaturated (``rhi`` > 1).
        - ``TLM_C``: float, liquid-hat threshold temperature [deg C].
        - ``TLC_C``: float, critical ambient temperature [deg C].
        - ``G``: float, mixing-line slope [Pa/K].
    """
    g = G(p_Pa, eta, q_fuel=q_fuel, ei_h2o=ei_h2o)
    tlm_k = solve_TLM(p_Pa, eta, q_fuel=q_fuel, ei_h2o=ei_h2o)
    rhw = rhi * thermo.e_i(T_K) / thermo.e_w(T_K)
    tlc_k = critical_T(rhw, tlm_k, g)
    return {
        "forms": T_K < tlc_k,
        "persists": rhi > 1.0,
        "TLM_C": tlm_k - _KELVIN_OFFSET,
        "TLC_C": tlc_k - _KELVIN_OFFSET,
        "G": g,
    }
```

## stratum/physics/thermo.py

```python
"""Thermodynamic baselines for Project STRATUM.

Implements the Murphy & Koop (2005) saturation vapor pressure
formulations for liquid water and ice, the exact analytic temperature
derivative of the liquid saturation curve, and ISA conversion from
flight level to ambient pressure.

References
----------
Murphy, D. M., & Koop, T. (2005). Review of the vapour pressures of ice
and supercooled water for atmospheric applications. Quarterly Journal of
the Royal Meteorological Society, 131(608), 1539-1565.

International Civil Aviation Organization (1993). Manual of the ICAO
Standard Atmosphere, 3rd ed., Doc 7488.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

__all__ = ["e_w", "e_i", "d_e_w_dT", "fl_to_p"]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# International Standard Atmosphere (ISA) constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

P0_PA: float = 101325.0  # sea-level pressure [Pa]
T0_K: float = 288.15  # sea-level temperature [K]
LAPSE_K_PER_M: float = 0.0065  # tropospheric lapse rate [K/m]
G0_M_PER_S2: float = 9.80665  # standard gravity [m/s^2]
R_UNIVERSAL_J_PER_MOL_K: float = 8.31446  # universal gas constant [J/(mol*K)]
M_AIR_KG_PER_MOL: float = 0.0289644  # molar mass of dry air [kg/mol]
R_AIR_J_PER_KG_K: float = R_UNIVERSAL_J_PER_MOL_K / M_AIR_KG_PER_MOL

H_TROPOPAUSE_M: float = 11000.0  # ISA tropopause altitude [m]
T_TROPOPAUSE_K: float = 216.65  # ISA tropopause temperature [K]

FT_TO_M: float = 0.3048  # feet to metres


def _ln_e_w_and_slope(T: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Return ``(ln e_w, d ln e_w / dT)`` for liquid water at ``T`` [K]."""
    tanh_term = math.tanh(0.0415 * (T - 218.8))
    inner = 53.878 - 1331.22 / T - 9.44523 * math.log(T) + 0.014025 * T
    d_inner = 1331.22 / T**2 - 9.44523 / T + 0.014025
    d_tanh = 0.0415 * (1.0 - tanh_term**2)

    ln_e = (
        54.842763
        - 6763.22 / T
        - 4.210 * math.log(T)
        + 0.000367 * T
        + tanh_term * inner
    )
    d_ln_e = (
        6763.22 / T**2
        - 4.210 / T
        + 0.000367
        + d_tanh * inner
        + tanh_term * d_inner
    )
    return ln_e, d_ln_e


def e_w(T: float) -> float:
    """Saturation vapor pressure over liquid water [Pa] at ``T`` [K].

    Implements Murphy & Koop (2005), eq. (10).
    """
    ln_e, _ = _ln_e_w_and_slope(T)
    return math.exp(ln_e)


def d_e_w_dT(T: float) -> float:
    """Exact analytic derivative d(e_w)/dT [Pa/K] at ``T`` [K].

    Differentiates the Murphy & Koop (2005) liquid curve analytically;
    no finite differences are used.
    """
    ln_e, d_ln_e = _ln_e_w_and_slope(T)
    return math.exp(ln_e) * d_ln_e


def e_i(T: float) -> float:
    """Saturation vapor pressure over ice [Pa] at ``T`` [K].

    Implements Murphy & Koop (2005), eq. (7).
    """
    ln_e = 9.550426 - 5723.265 / T + 3.53068 * math.log(T) - 0.00728332 * T
    return math.exp(ln_e)


def fl_to_p(fl_hundred_ft: float) -> float:
    """Convert a flight level to ISA ambient pressure [hPa].

    ``fl_hundred_ft`` is the flight level in hundreds of feet (e.g. 250
    for FL250). Uses the ISA tropospheric lapse rate up to 11 km and the
    isothermal layer above it.
    """
    h_m = fl_hundred_ft * 100.0 * FT_TO_M
    exponent = G0_M_PER_S2 / (R_AIR_J_PER_KG_K * LAPSE_K_PER_M)

    if h_m <= H_TROPOPAUSE_M:
        T_K = T0_K - LAPSE_K_PER_M * h_m
        p_pa = P0_PA * (T_K / T0_K) ** exponent
    else:
        p_trop_pa = P0_PA * (T_TROPOPAUSE_K / T0_K) ** exponent
        p_pa = p_trop_pa * math.exp(
            -G0_M_PER_S2 * (h_m - H_TROPOPAUSE_M) / (R_AIR_J_PER_KG_K * T_TROPOPAUSE_K)
        )
    return p_pa / 100.0
```

```python
"""End-to-end pipeline package (Step 8).

Assembles the canonical feature matrix along cleaned ADS-B trajectories
and evaluates a full flight corridor against the calibrated persistence
model plus the counterfactual decision layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from stratum.pipeline.build_features import (
    CLOUD_ICE_COVER_THRESHOLD,
    HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA,
    TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M,
    extract_features_for_trajectory,
    pressure_to_altitude_m,
    specific_humidity_to_rhi,

from stratum.pipeline.runner import (

__all__ = [
```

## stratum/pipeline/build_features.py

```python
"""Feature assembly along cleaned ADS-B trajectories (Step 8).

For every trajectory point the assembler converts barometric altitude to a
pressure level, interpolates the ambient ERA5 state at the target level
and at +/- 25 hPa humidity offsets, evaluates the Schmidt-Appleman
thresholds, derives the remaining atmospheric features, and produces one
row in the canonical feature order of :mod:`stratum.model.features`.

Reference assumptions (documented, not measured):

- the humidity offsets sample the vertical structure of humidity: the
  "above" sample uses the lower pressure ``p - 25 hPa``, the "below"
  sample the higher pressure ``p + 25 hPa``;
- the tropopause is taken at 16 km (the standard tropical/subtropical
  level near 100 hPa), so ``dist_to_tropopause_m`` is signed relative to
  it;
- ``cloud_ice`` is a proxy flag (cloud cover above 0.5) because the
  canonical ERA5 variable set does not carry cloud ice content;
- turbulence enters through the gradient Richardson number computed on
  the two bounding layers, radiative geometry through the solar zenith
  angle, and engine microphysics through the designator lookup in
  :mod:`stratum.physics.diagnostics`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import xarray as xr

from stratum.io.era5 import interpolate_met_to_segment
from stratum.model.features import extract_features_row, feature_vector
from stratum.physics import G, critical_T, e_i, e_w, eps, fl_to_p, solve_TLM
from stratum.physics import thermo
from stratum.physics.diagnostics import (
    compute_richardson_number,
    compute_solar_zenith_angle,
    get_aircraft_params,
)

__all__ = [
    "TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M",
    "HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA",
    "CLOUD_ICE_COVER_THRESHOLD",
    "specific_humidity_to_rhi",
    "pressure_to_altitude_m",
    "extract_features_for_trajectory",
]

#: Assumed tropical/subtropical tropopause altitude [m] (~100 hPa).
TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M: float = 16000.0

#: Vertical humidity sampling offset [hPa] for the structure features.
HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA: float = 25.0

#: Cloud cover threshold used as the ``cloud_ice`` proxy flag.
CLOUD_ICE_COVER_THRESHOLD: float = 0.5

_REQUIRED_TRAJECTORY_COLUMNS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "time",
    "lat",
    "lon",
    "baro_alt_ft",
)

_KELVIN_OFFSET: float = 273.15


def specific_humidity_to_rhi(
    specific_humidity: float,
    pressure_pa: float,
    temperature_k: float,
) -> float:
    """Convert specific humidity to relative humidity over ice [-].

    Vapor pressure follows ``e = q p / (eps + (1 - eps) q)`` and RHi is
    ``e / e_i(T)`` with the Murphy & Koop ice curve. Returns ``nan`` for
    non-finite inputs.
    """
    if not (
        math.isfinite(specific_humidity)
        and math.isfinite(pressure_pa)
        and math.isfinite(temperature_k)
    ) or pressure_pa <= 0.0:
        return float("nan")
    vapor_pressure = specific_humidity * pressure_pa / (
        eps + (1.0 - eps) * specific_humidity
    )
    return vapor_pressure / e_i(temperature_k)


def pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa: float) -> float:
    """Invert the ISA pressure-altitude relation [m] for ``pressure_pa``.

    Uses the tropospheric lapse below the 11 km tropopause and the
    isothermal layer above it (ICAO standard atmosphere).

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        For non-positive or non-finite pressure.
    """
    if not math.isfinite(pressure_pa) or pressure_pa <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f"pressure_pa must be positive, got {pressure_pa!r}")

    exponent = thermo.G0_M_PER_S2 / (
        thermo.R_AIR_J_PER_KG_K * thermo.LAPSE_K_PER_M
    )
    pressure_tropopause = thermo.P0_PA * (
        thermo.T_TROPOPAUSE_K / thermo.T0_K
    ) ** exponent
    if pressure_pa >= pressure_tropopause:
        temperature_k = thermo.T0_K * (pressure_pa / thermo.P0_PA) ** (
            1.0 / exponent
        )
        return (thermo.T0_K - temperature_k) / thermo.LAPSE_K_PER_M
    return thermo.H_TROPOPAUSE_M + (
        thermo.R_AIR_J_PER_KG_K
        * thermo.T_TROPOPAUSE_K
        / thermo.G0_M_PER_S2
    ) * math.log(pressure_tropopause / pressure_pa)


def extract_features_for_trajectory(
    df_trajectory: pd.DataFrame,
    ds_met: xr.Dataset,
    aircraft_type: str = "A320",
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    """Assemble the canonical feature matrix along a trajectory.

    Parameters
    ----------
    df_trajectory : pandas.DataFrame
        Cleaned ADS-B points; requires ``time``, ``lat``, ``lon`` and
        ``baro_alt_ft``. Optional ``icao24``/``callsign`` columns are
        echoed into the metadata.
    ds_met : xarray.Dataset
        ERA5 dataset with the canonical variables, e.g. from
        :func:`stratum.io.era5.load_era5_dataset`.
    aircraft_type : str, optional
        ICAO designator used to look up the engine parameters
        (propulsion efficiency ``eta`` and nvPM number emission index)
        from :data:`stratum.physics.diagnostics.AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE`,
        by default ``"A320"``.

    Returns
    -------
    tuple of (numpy.ndarray, list of dict)
        - feature matrix ``X`` of shape ``(N, len(FEATURE_NAMES))`` in
          canonical order;
        - one metadata dictionary per row (time, position, flight level,
          ambient state, SAC and diagnostic values) for reporting and
          audits.

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        If required columns are missing, the frame is empty, or a row
        carries non-finite position/altitude or a missing time.
    """
    missing = [
        name
        for name in _REQUIRED_TRAJECTORY_COLUMNS
        if name not in df_trajectory.columns
    ]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"trajectory frame is missing columns: {missing}")
    if df_trajectory.empty:
        raise ValueError("trajectory frame is empty")

    engine = get_aircraft_params(aircraft_type)
    engine_eta = float(engine["eta"])
    log_soot_ei_n = math.log10(float(engine["ei_n"]))

    times = pd.to_datetime(df_trajectory["time"], utc=True)
    vectors: list[np.ndarray] = []
    metadata: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for position in range(len(df_trajectory)):
        record = df_trajectory.iloc[position]
        lat = float(record["lat"])
        lon = float(record["lon"])
        altitude_ft = float(record["baro_alt_ft"])
        if not all(math.isfinite(value) for value in (lat, lon, altitude_ft)):
            raise ValueError(
                f"row {position} has non-finite lat/lon/baro_alt_ft; clean the "
                "trajectory first"
            )
        timestamp = times.iloc[position]
        if pd.isna(timestamp):
            raise ValueError(f"row {position} has a missing timestamp")

        flight_level = altitude_ft / 100.0
        pressure_pa = fl_to_p(flight_level) * 100.0
        altitude_m = pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa)
        offset_pa = HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA * 100.0

        met = interpolate_met_to_segment(ds_met, lat, lon, pressure_pa, timestamp)
        met_above = interpolate_met_to_segment(
            ds_met, lat, lon, pressure_pa - offset_pa, timestamp
        )
        met_below = interpolate_met_to_segment(
            ds_met, lat, lon, pressure_pa + offset_pa, timestamp
        )

        temperature_k = met["temperature"]
        rhi = specific_humidity_to_rhi(
            met["specific_humidity"], pressure_pa, temperature_k
        )
        rhi_above = specific_humidity_to_rhi(
            met_above["specific_humidity"],
            pressure_pa - offset_pa,
            met_above["temperature"],
        )
        rhi_below = specific_humidity_to_rhi(
            met_below["specific_humidity"],
            pressure_pa + offset_pa,
            met_below["temperature"],
        )
        rhi_grad = rhi_above - rhi_below

        tlm_k = solve_TLM(pressure_pa, engine_eta)
        if math.isfinite(temperature_k) and math.isfinite(rhi):
            rhw = max(rhi * e_i(temperature_k) / e_w(temperature_k), 1.0e-9)
            tlc_k = critical_T(rhw, tlm_k, G(pressure_pa, engine_eta))
            sac_forms = temperature_k < tlc_k
        else:
            tlc_k = float("nan")
            sac_forms = False

        delta_u = met_above["u_component_of_wind"] - met_below["u_component_of_wind"]
        delta_v = met_above["v_component_of_wind"] - met_below["v_component_of_wind"]
        altitude_above_m = pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa - offset_pa)
        altitude_below_m = pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa + offset_pa)
        delta_z = abs(altitude_below_m - altitude_above_m)
        wind_shear = math.hypot(delta_u, delta_v) / delta_z

        richardson_inputs = (
            met_above["temperature"],
            met_below["temperature"],
            met_above["u_component_of_wind"],
            met_below["u_component_of_wind"],
            met_above["v_component_of_wind"],
            met_below["v_component_of_wind"],
        )
        if all(math.isfinite(value) for value in richardson_inputs):
            richardson_number = compute_richardson_number(
                temp_above_k=met_above["temperature"],
                temp_below_k=met_below["temperature"],
                u_above=met_above["u_component_of_wind"],
                u_below=met_below["u_component_of_wind"],
                v_above=met_above["v_component_of_wind"],
                v_below=met_below["v_component_of_wind"],
                alt_above_m=altitude_above_m,
                alt_below_m=altitude_below_m,
                p_ambient_pa=pressure_pa,
            )
        else:
            # Neutral laminar fallback keeps the feature matrix NaN-free
            # when a bounding layer is outside the ERA5 domain.
            richardson_number = 1.0

        solar_zenith_deg = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
            timestamp.tz_convert("UTC").to_pydatetime(), lat, lon
        )

        cloud_cover = met["fraction_of_cloud_cover"]
        if math.isfinite(cloud_cover):
            cloud_ice = 1.0 if cloud_cover > CLOUD_ICE_COVER_THRESHOLD else 0.0
        else:
            cloud_ice = float("nan")

        local_solar_time = (
            float(timestamp.hour)
            + float(timestamp.minute) / 60.0
            + lon / 15.0
        ) % 24.0

        row = extract_features_row(
            rhi=rhi,
            rhi_above=rhi_above,
            rhi_below=rhi_below,
            rhi_grad=rhi_grad,
            temp_k=temperature_k,
            tlm_k=tlm_k,
            tlc_k=tlc_k,
            pressure_pa=pressure_pa,
            cloud_ice=cloud_ice,
            high_cloud_fraction=cloud_cover,
            vertical_velocity_omega=met["vertical_velocity"],
            vertical_wind_shear=wind_shear,
            dist_to_tropopause_m=altitude_m - TROPOPAUSE_ALTITUDE_M,
            lat=lat,
            lon=lon,
            month=float(timestamp.month),
            local_solar_time_hours=local_solar_time,
            richardson_number=richardson_number,
            solar_zenith_deg=solar_zenith_deg,
            engine_efficiency_eta=engine_eta,
            log_soot_ei_n=log_soot_ei_n,
        )
        vectors.append(feature_vector(row))
        metadata.append(
            {
                "row_index": position,
                "icao24": str(record["icao24"]) if "icao24" in df_trajectory else "",
                "callsign": (
                    str(record["callsign"]) if "callsign" in df_trajectory else ""
                ),
                "time_utc": timestamp.isoformat(),
                "lat": lat,
                "lon": lon,
                "flight_level": flight_level,
                "pressure_pa": pressure_pa,
                "altitude_m": altitude_m,
                "temperature_k": temperature_k,
                "rhi": rhi,
                "rhi_above": rhi_above,
                "rhi_below": rhi_below,
                "tlm_c": tlm_k - _KELVIN_OFFSET,
                "tlc_c": tlc_k - _KELVIN_OFFSET,
                "richardson_number": richardson_number,
                "solar_zenith_deg": solar_zenith_deg,
                "engine_efficiency_eta": engine_eta,
                "log_soot_ei_n": log_soot_ei_n,
                "sac_forms": bool(sac_forms),
                "ambient_ice_supersaturated": bool(rhi > 1.0),
            }
        )

    return np.vstack(vectors), metadata
```

## stratum/pipeline/runner.py

```python
"""Corridor-level pipeline orchestration (Step 8).

Ties the whole STRATUM chain together for a single flight: feature
assembly along the cleaned ADS-B trajectory, calibrated persistence
probability, per-segment counterfactual evaluation, and aggregation into
an actionable reroute advisory. Consecutive flagged segments are grouped
into one *event* so the same avoided contrail is not counted twice.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import xarray as xr

from stratum.decide.counterfactual import (
    ABSTAIN_THRESHOLD,
    AltitudeDecision,
    evaluate_altitude_candidates,
)
from stratum.model.calibrate import CalibratedModel
from stratum.pipeline.build_features import extract_features_for_trajectory

__all__ = [
    "PERSISTENT_PROBABILITY_THRESHOLD",
    "evaluate_corridor_flight",
]

#: Calibrated probability at or above which a segment counts as
#: "persistent" in the flight statistics (the decision layer activation
#: uses :data:`stratum.decide.counterfactual.ABSTAIN_THRESHOLD` instead).
PERSISTENT_PROBABILITY_THRESHOLD: float = 0.50


def evaluate_corridor_flight(
    df_trajectory: pd.DataFrame,
    ds_met: xr.Dataset,
    model: CalibratedModel,
    aircraft_type: str = "A320",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Evaluate one flight corridor end to end.

    Parameters
    ----------
    df_trajectory : pandas.DataFrame
        Cleaned ADS-B trajectory (see
        :func:`stratum.pipeline.build_features.extract_features_for_trajectory`).
    ds_met : xarray.Dataset
        ERA5 dataset with the canonical variables.
    model : CalibratedModel
        Trained LightGBM model with isotonic calibration; must expose
        ``predict_proba(X) -> (N, 2)``.
    aircraft_type : str, optional
        Aircraft type for the fuel penalty model, by default ``"A320"``.

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, Any]
        Aggregated flight report with at least:

        - ``total_segments`` : number of trajectory points evaluated;
        - ``contrail_forming_segments`` : segments where the SAC criterion
          is satisfied (physics);
        - ``contrail_persistent_segments`` : segments with calibrated
          ``P(persist) >= 0.50``;
        - ``total_avoidable_warming_j`` : avoided contrail effective
          forcing summed over reroute events [J];
        - ``recommended_reroutes`` : one structured advisory per
          contiguous flagged run (level change, fuel/CO2 delta, avoided
          warming, benefit-to-cost ratio);
        - ``segments`` : per-segment atmospheric and decision records.

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        Propagated from feature extraction for malformed trajectories.
    """
    features, metadata = extract_features_for_trajectory(
        df_trajectory, ds_met, aircraft_type
    )
    probabilities = np.asarray(model.predict_proba(features), dtype=float)[:, 1]

    forming_segments = 0
    persistent_segments = 0
    flagged_indices: list[int] = []
    decisions: dict[int, AltitudeDecision] = {}
    segments: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for index, info in enumerate(metadata):
        probability = float(probabilities[index])
        if info["sac_forms"]:
            forming_segments += 1
        if probability >= PERSISTENT_PROBABILITY_THRESHOLD:
            persistent_segments += 1

        decision: AltitudeDecision | None = None
        if probability >= ABSTAIN_THRESHOLD:
            decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(
                probability, float(info["flight_level"]), aircraft_type
            )
            decisions[index] = decision
            flagged_indices.append(index)

        segments.append(
            {
                "segment_index": index,
                "time_utc": info["time_utc"],
                "lat": info["lat"],
                "lon": info["lon"],
                "flight_level": info["flight_level"],
                "pressure_pa": info["pressure_pa"],
                "temperature_c": info["temperature_k"] - 273.15,
                "rhi": info["rhi"],
                "tlm_c": info["tlm_c"],
                "tlc_c": info["tlc_c"],
                "sac_forms": info["sac_forms"],
                "ambient_ice_supersaturated": info["ambient_ice_supersaturated"],
                "p_persist": probability,
                "decision_status": decision["status"] if decision else None,
                "recommended_fl": decision["recommended_fl"] if decision else None,
                "delta_fuel_kg": decision["delta_fuel_kg"] if decision else None,
                "delta_co2_kg": decision["delta_co2_kg"] if decision else None,
                "avoided_contrail_ef_j": (
                    decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] if decision else None
                ),
            }
        )

    # Group consecutive flagged segments into advisory events.
    groups: list[list[int]] = []
    for index in flagged_indices:
        if groups and index == groups[-1][-1] + 1:
            groups[-1].append(index)
        else:
            groups.append([index])

    reroutes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for group in groups:
        representative = max(group, key=lambda item: probabilities[item])
        decision = decisions[representative]
        if decision["status"] != "eligible":
            continue
        reroutes.append(
            {
                "event_index": len(reroutes),
                "segment_index": representative,
                "time_utc": metadata[representative]["time_utc"],
                "segments_in_event": len(group),
                "p_persist": float(probabilities[representative]),
                "baseline_fl": float(decision["baseline_fl"]),
                "recommended_fl": float(decision["recommended_fl"]),
                "direction": (
                    "climb"
                    if float(decision["recommended_fl"]) > float(decision["baseline_fl"])
                    else "descent"
                ),
                "delta_fuel_kg": float(decision["delta_fuel_kg"]),
                "delta_co2_kg": float(decision["delta_co2_kg"]),
                "avoided_contrail_ef_j": float(decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"]),
                "benefit_to_cost_ratio": float(decision["benefit_to_cost_ratio"]),
            }
        )

    total_avoidable_warming_j = float(
        sum(entry["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] for entry in reroutes)
    )
    total_extra_fuel_kg = float(sum(entry["delta_fuel_kg"] for entry in reroutes))
    total_extra_co2_kg = float(sum(entry["delta_co2_kg"] for entry in reroutes))

    return {
        "total_segments": int(len(metadata)),
        "contrail_forming_segments": int(forming_segments),
        "contrail_persistent_segments": int(persistent_segments),
        "flagged_segments": int(len(flagged_indices)),
        "total_avoidable_warming_j": total_avoidable_warming_j,
        "total_extra_fuel_kg": total_extra_fuel_kg,
        "total_extra_co2_kg": total_extra_co2_kg,
        "recommended_reroutes": reroutes,
        "segments": segments,
    }
```

## stratum/routes/corridor.py

```python
"""Indian flight-corridor generation and route-level contrail screening.

Provides ICAO airport coordinates, great-circle waypoint interpolation at
a prescribed flight level, and a reduction of per-waypoint
Schmidt-Appleman criterion (SAC) evaluations to route-level statistics.

Waypoints carry ambient pressure in Pa (converted from the ISA pressure
in hPa returned by :func:`stratum.physics.thermo.fl_to_p`) so they can be
fed directly to :func:`stratum.physics.sac.evaluate_sac`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Sequence, TypedDict

from stratum.physics import thermo
from stratum.physics.sac import evaluate_sac

__all__ = [
    "AIRPORTS",
    "Waypoint",
    "RouteProfileResult",
    "piecewise_profile",
    "great_circle_waypoints",
    "evaluate_route_profile",
]

#: ICAO location indicator -> (latitude [deg N], longitude [deg E]).
AIRPORTS: dict[str, tuple[float, float]] = {
    "VIDP": (28.5665, 77.1031),  # Delhi, Indira Gandhi International
    "VABB": (19.0896, 72.8656),  # Mumbai, Chhatrapati Shivaji Maharaj International
    "VOBL": (13.1986, 77.7066),  # Bengaluru, Kempegowda International
    "VECC": (22.6547, 88.4467),  # Kolkata, Netaji Subhas Chandra Bose International
    "VAAH": (23.0772, 72.6347),  # Ahmedabad, Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel International
}

_KELVIN_OFFSET: float = 273.15
_HPA_PER_PA: float = 100.0
_RHI_MAX: float = 2.0


class Waypoint(TypedDict):
    """A single corridor waypoint.

    Attributes
    ----------
    lat : float
        Latitude [deg N].
    lon : float
        Longitude [deg E].
    fl : float
        Flight level [hundreds of feet].
    p_Pa : float
        ISA ambient pressure [Pa].
    """

    lat: float
    lon: float
    fl: float
    p_Pa: float


class RouteProfileResult(TypedDict):
    """Route-level summary of per-waypoint SAC evaluations.

    Attributes
    ----------
    total_waypoints : int
        Number of evaluated waypoints.
    formation_count : int
        Waypoints where the SAC holds (contrail forms).
    persistence_count : int
        Waypoints that are ice-supersaturated (RHi > 1).
    formation_pct : float
        ``formation_count`` as a percentage of ``total_waypoints``.
    persistence_pct : float
        ``persistence_count`` as a percentage of ``total_waypoints``.
    details : list of dict
        Per-waypoint SAC dictionaries as returned by
        :func:`stratum.physics.sac.evaluate_sac`.
    """

    total_waypoints: int
    formation_count: int
    persistence_count: int
    formation_pct: float
    persistence_pct: float
    details: list[dict[str, float | bool]]


def piecewise_profile(
    knots: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], num_points: int
) -> list[float]:
    """Sample a piecewise-linear profile given ``(fraction, value)`` knots.

    Parameters
    ----------
    knots : sequence of (float, float)
        Profile control points. Fractions are route positions in [0, 1]
        and must be strictly increasing; values are linearly
        interpolated between knots and clamped outside the knot range.
    num_points : int
        Number of equally spaced samples over [0, 1].

    Returns
    -------
    list of float
        Sampled profile values, starting at the first knot value and
        ending at the last knot value.
    """
    if num_points < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"num_points must be >= 2, got {num_points}")
    if not knots:
        raise ValueError("knots must contain at least one (fraction, value) pair")
    fractions = [fraction for fraction, _ in knots]
    if any(b <= a for a, b in zip(fractions, fractions[1:])):
        raise ValueError(f"knot fractions must be strictly increasing, got {fractions}")

    values: list[float] = []
    segment = 0
    for i in range(num_points):
        t = i / (num_points - 1)
        while segment < len(knots) - 2 and t > knots[segment + 1][0]:
            segment += 1
        f0, v0 = knots[segment]
        if segment + 1 < len(knots):
            f1, v1 = knots[segment + 1]
        else:
            f1, v1 = f0, v0
        if t <= f0:
            values.append(v0)
        elif t >= f1:
            values.append(v1)
        else:
            values.append(v0 + (v1 - v0) * (t - f0) / (f1 - f0))
    return values


def _unit_vector(lat_rad: float, lon_rad: float) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """Return the Cartesian unit vector for spherical angles [rad]."""
    cos_lat = math.cos(lat_rad)
    return (
        cos_lat * math.cos(lon_rad),
        cos_lat * math.sin(lon_rad),
        math.sin(lat_rad),
    )


def _to_latlon(x: float, y: float, z: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Convert a Cartesian direction to (latitude, longitude) [deg]."""
    lat_deg = math.degrees(math.atan2(z, math.sqrt(x * x + y * y)))
    lon_deg = math.degrees(math.atan2(y, x))
    return lat_deg, lon_deg


def great_circle_waypoints(
    coord1: tuple[float, float],
    coord2: tuple[float, float],
    num_points: int = 25,
    fl: float = 340,
) -> list[Waypoint]:
    """Interpolate ``num_points`` great-circle waypoints between two points.

    Endpoints are reproduced exactly; intermediate points use spherical
    linear interpolation (slerp) of the unit direction vectors. Every
    waypoint carries the ISA pressure for flight level ``fl``.

    Parameters
    ----------
    coord1, coord2 : (float, float)
        Start and end coordinates as (latitude [deg N], longitude [deg E]).
    num_points : int, optional
        Total number of waypoints including both endpoints, by default 25.
    fl : float, optional
        Flight level in hundreds of feet, by default 340.

    Returns
    -------
    list of Waypoint
        ``num_points`` dictionaries with keys ``lat``, ``lon``, ``fl`` and
        ``p_Pa`` (ISA pressure [Pa]).
    """
    if num_points < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"num_points must be >= 2, got {num_points}")

    lat1_rad, lon1_rad = math.radians(coord1[0]), math.radians(coord1[1])
    lat2_rad, lon2_rad = math.radians(coord2[0]), math.radians(coord2[1])
    v1 = _unit_vector(lat1_rad, lon1_rad)
    v2 = _unit_vector(lat2_rad, lon2_rad)

    dot = min(1.0, max(-1.0, sum(a * b for a, b in zip(v1, v2))))
    omega = math.acos(dot)
    p_pa = thermo.fl_to_p(fl) * _HPA_PER_PA

    waypoints: list[Waypoint] = []
    for i in range(num_points):
        if i == 0:
            lat_deg, lon_deg = coord1[0], coord1[1]
        elif i == num_points - 1:
            lat_deg, lon_deg = coord2[0], coord2[1]
        else:
            t = i / (num_points - 1)
            if omega < 1e-12:
                x = (1.0 - t) * v1[0] + t * v2[0]
                y = (1.0 - t) * v1[1] + t * v2[1]
                z = (1.0 - t) * v1[2] + t * v2[2]
            else:
                scale = math.sin(omega)
                a = math.sin((1.0 - t) * omega) / scale
                b = math.sin(t * omega) / scale
                x = a * v1[0] + b * v2[0]
                y = a * v1[1] + b * v2[1]
                z = a * v1[2] + b * v2[2]
            norm = math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z)
            lat_deg, lon_deg = _to_latlon(x / norm, y / norm, z / norm)
        waypoints.append(
            {"lat": lat_deg, "lon": lon_deg, "fl": float(fl), "p_Pa": p_pa}
        )
    return waypoints


def evaluate_route_profile(
    waypoints: Sequence[Waypoint],
    temp_c_list: Sequence[float],
    rhi_list: Sequence[float],
    eta: float = 0.30,
) -> RouteProfileResult:
    """Evaluate the Schmidt-Appleman criterion along a route profile.

    Parameters
    ----------
    waypoints : sequence of Waypoint
        Waypoints from :func:`great_circle_waypoints`.
    temp_c_list : sequence of float
        Ambient temperatures [deg C], one per waypoint.
    rhi_list : sequence of float
        Ambient relative humidities over ice [-], one per waypoint,
        each within [0.0, 2.0].
    eta : float, optional
        Engine efficiency (0-1), by default 0.30.

    Returns
    -------
    RouteProfileResult
        Route-level counts, percentages and per-waypoint SAC details.

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        If the profile list lengths differ from the waypoint count or a
        RHi value lies outside [0.0, 2.0].
    """
    total = len(waypoints)
    if len(temp_c_list) != total or len(rhi_list) != total:
        raise ValueError(
            "profile lengths must match waypoint count: "
            f"{total} waypoints, {len(temp_c_list)} temperatures, "
            f"{len(rhi_list)} RHi values"
        )

    details: list[dict[str, float | bool]] = []
    formation_count = 0
    persistence_count = 0
    for waypoint, temp_c, rhi in zip(waypoints, temp_c_list, rhi_list):
        if not 0.0 <= rhi <= _RHI_MAX:
            raise ValueError(f"RHi must lie in [0.0, {_RHI_MAX}], got {rhi!r}")
        detail = evaluate_sac(
            float(waypoint["p_Pa"]), float(temp_c) + _KELVIN_OFFSET, float(rhi), eta=eta
        )
        details.append(detail)
        if detail["forms"]:
            formation_count += 1
        if detail["persists"]:
            persistence_count += 1

    return {
        "total_waypoints": total,
        "formation_count": formation_count,
        "persistence_count": persistence_count,
        "formation_pct": 100.0 * formation_count / total,
        "persistence_pct": 100.0 * persistence_count / total,
        "details": details,
    }
```

## stratum_model_progress.html

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Project STRATUM - Corridor Flight Report</title>
<style>
  :root {
    --navy: #0f2440;
    --navy-soft: #1c3a5e;
    --ink: #16202e;
    --muted: #5c6b7e;
    --line: #e3e8ef;
    --surface: #f6f8fb;
    --card: #ffffff;
    --green: #0f7a55;
    --green-soft: #e3f4ec;
    --amber: #9a6200;
    --amber-soft: #fdf0d5;
    --red: #b3261e;
    --red-soft: #fbe4e2;
    --blue: #1559a6;
    --blue-soft: #e4eefb;
  }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  body {
    margin: 0;
    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto,
      "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
    color: var(--ink);
    background: var(--surface);
    line-height: 1.5;
  }
  .page { max-width: 1080px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 32px 24px 56px; }
  header {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--navy) 0%, var(--navy-soft) 100%);
    color: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 14px;
    padding: 28px 32px;
    margin-bottom: 24px;
  }
  header h1 { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 26px; letter-spacing: 0.2px; }
  header .subtitle { margin: 0 0 18px; color: #c8d6e8; font-size: 14px; }
  .badges { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
  .pill {
    display: inline-block;
    padding: 4px 11px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: 600;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28);
    color: #f2f7fd;
  }
  .pill.ok { background: rgba(52, 199, 138, 0.22); border-color: rgba(52, 199, 138, 0.5); }
  section { margin-bottom: 28px; }
  h2 {
    font-size: 15px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    color: var(--muted);
    margin: 0 0 12px;
  }
  .meta-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr));
    gap: 10px 24px;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 18px 22px;
    font-size: 14px;
  }
  .meta-grid dt { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; margin: 0; }
  .meta-grid dd { margin: 2px 0 8px; font-weight: 600; }
  .kpis { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); gap: 14px; }
  .kpi {
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 18px 20px;
  }
  .kpi .kpi-label { font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; color: var(--muted); }
  .kpi .kpi-value { font-size: 26px; font-weight: 700; margin: 6px 0 4px; }
  .kpi .kpi-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }
  .kpi .kpi-value.ok { color: var(--green); }
  .advisory {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--navy) 0%, #234b7a 100%);
    color: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 14px;
    padding: 24px 28px;
  }
  .advisory h3 { margin: 0 0 14px; font-size: 17px; letter-spacing: 0.3px; }
  .advisory-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }
  .advisory-label { font-size: 12px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.06em; color: #b9cbe2; }
  .advisory-value { font-size: 28px; font-weight: 700; margin: 4px 0 2px; }
  .advisory-sub { font-size: 13px; color: #c8d6e8; }
  .advisory-note { margin: 16px 0 0; font-size: 13px; color: #d7e3f2; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18); padding-top: 12px; }
  table {
    width: 100%;
    border-collapse: collapse;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    overflow: hidden;
    font-size: 13.5px;
  }
  thead th {
    text-align: left;
    background: #eef2f8;
    color: #3d4d61;
    font-size: 12px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    padding: 10px 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  }
  tbody td { padding: 8px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eef1f6; }
  tbody tr:nth-child(even) { background: #fafcfe; }
  tbody tr:last-child td { border-bottom: none; }
  td.num { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .badge { display: inline-block; padding: 2px 10px; border-radius: 999px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; }
  .badge-clear { background: var(--green-soft); color: var(--green); }
  .badge-aerodynamic { background: var(--amber-soft); color: var(--amber); }
  .badge-persistent { background: var(--red-soft); color: var(--red); }
  footer { margin-top: 32px; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--muted); border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 14px; }
  code { background: #eef2f8; padding: 1px 6px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12.5px; }
</style>
</head>
```
