## tests/test_corridors.py

```python
"""Tests for Indian corridor generation and route-level SAC screening."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from stratum.physics import thermo
from stratum.routes import corridor

FL340: float = 340.0
NUM_POINTS: int = 25


def test_airport_coordinates_registered() -> None:
    """ICAO coordinates match the STRATUM reference values."""
    assert corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"] == (28.5665, 77.1031)
    assert corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"] == (19.0896, 72.8656)
    assert corridor.AIRPORTS["VOBL"] == (13.1986, 77.7066)
    assert corridor.AIRPORTS["VECC"] == (22.6547, 88.4467)
    assert corridor.AIRPORTS["VAAH"] == (23.0772, 72.6347)


def test_great_circle_endpoints_length_and_pressure() -> None:
    """Default track has 25 points, exact endpoints and ISA FL340 pressure."""
    coord1 = corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"]
    coord2 = corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"]
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(coord1, coord2)

    assert len(waypoints) == NUM_POINTS
    assert (waypoints[0]["lat"], waypoints[0]["lon"]) == coord1
    assert (waypoints[-1]["lat"], waypoints[-1]["lon"]) == coord2

    p_expected = thermo.fl_to_p(FL340) * 100.0
    for waypoint in waypoints:
        assert waypoint["fl"] == FL340
        assert waypoint["p_Pa"] == pytest.approx(p_expected, rel=1e-12)
        assert 19.0 <= waypoint["lat"] <= 29.0
        assert 72.0 <= waypoint["lon"] <= 78.0


def test_great_circle_custom_num_points_and_fl() -> None:
    """Custom point count and flight level are honoured."""
    coord1 = corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"]
    coord2 = corridor.AIRPORTS["VOBL"]
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(coord1, coord2, num_points=2, fl=380)

    assert len(waypoints) == 2
    assert (waypoints[0]["lat"], waypoints[0]["lon"]) == coord1
    assert (waypoints[1]["lat"], waypoints[1]["lon"]) == coord2
    assert all(waypoint["fl"] == 380.0 for waypoint in waypoints)
    assert all(
        waypoint["p_Pa"] == pytest.approx(thermo.fl_to_p(380.0) * 100.0, rel=1e-12)
        for waypoint in waypoints
    )


def test_great_circle_latitude_decreases_delhi_to_mumbai() -> None:
    """A southbound great-circle leg tracks monotonically decreasing latitude."""
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(
        corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"], corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"]
    )
    latitudes = [waypoint["lat"] for waypoint in waypoints]
    assert all(a > b for a, b in zip(latitudes, latitudes[1:]))


def test_great_circle_requires_two_points() -> None:
    """Fewer than two waypoints is rejected."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        corridor.great_circle_waypoints(
            corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"], corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"], num_points=1
        )


def test_evaluate_route_profile_validates_lengths() -> None:
    """Profile lists must match the waypoint count."""
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(
        corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"], corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"]
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        corridor.evaluate_route_profile(waypoints, [-50.0, -49.0], [0.5] * NUM_POINTS)


def test_vabb_vobl_winter_zero_persistence() -> None:
    """Warm tropical leg: T > T_LC everywhere, exactly 0% persistence."""
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(
        corridor.AIRPORTS["VABB"], corridor.AIRPORTS["VOBL"]
    )
    temps = corridor.piecewise_profile(((0.0, -38.0), (1.0, -34.0)), NUM_POINTS)
    rhi_values = corridor.piecewise_profile(((0.0, 0.30), (1.0, 0.75)), NUM_POINTS)

    result = corridor.evaluate_route_profile(waypoints, temps, rhi_values)

    assert result["persistence_count"] == 0
    assert result["persistence_pct"] == 0.0
    assert result["formation_count"] == 0
    for detail, temp_c in zip(result["details"], temps):
        assert detail["forms"] is False
        assert float(detail["TLC_C"]) < temp_c  # ambient warmer than T_LC


def test_vidp_vecc_winter_persistence_above_40pct() -> None:
    """Moist winter jet leg: ice-supersaturated over 40% of waypoints."""
    waypoints = corridor.great_circle_waypoints(
        corridor.AIRPORTS["VIDP"], corridor.AIRPORTS["VECC"]
    )
    temps = corridor.piecewise_profile(((0.0, -54.0), (1.0, -47.0)), NUM_POINTS)
    rhi_values = corridor.piecewise_profile(
        ((0.0, 0.65), (0.55, 1.00), (1.0, 1.25)), NUM_POINTS
    )

    result = corridor.evaluate_route_profile(waypoints, temps, rhi_values)

    assert result["persistence_pct"] > 40.0
    assert result["formation_count"] >= result["persistence_count"]
    assert result["total_waypoints"] == NUM_POINTS
```

## tests/test_decide.py

```python
"""Tests for the counterfactual decision layer (Step 6)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from stratum.decide import (
    CO2_PER_KG_FUEL,
    calculate_net_climate_benefit,
    estimate_fuel_penalty,
    evaluate_altitude_candidates,
)

WINDOW_SECONDS = 20.0 * 60.0
CRUISE_BURN_KG_S = 0.65


def test_co2_conversion_100_kg_fuel() -> None:
    assert CO2_PER_KG_FUEL * 100.0 == pytest.approx(316.0)


def test_fuel_penalty_co2_is_consistent() -> None:
    penalty = estimate_fuel_penalty("A320", 350.0, 370.0)
    assert penalty["delta_co2_kg"] == pytest.approx(
        penalty["delta_fuel_kg"] * CO2_PER_KG_FUEL
    )


def test_climb_penalty_midpoint() -> None:
    penalty = estimate_fuel_penalty("A320", 350.0, 370.0)
    assert penalty["penalty_pct"] == pytest.approx(2.0)
    assert penalty["delta_fuel_kg"] == pytest.approx(
        0.020 * CRUISE_BURN_KG_S * WINDOW_SECONDS
    )


def test_descent_penalty_midpoint() -> None:
    penalty = estimate_fuel_penalty("B737", 350.0, 330.0)
    assert penalty["penalty_pct"] == pytest.approx(1.15)
    assert penalty["delta_fuel_kg"] == pytest.approx(
        0.0115 * CRUISE_BURN_KG_S * WINDOW_SECONDS
    )


def test_level_change_is_free() -> None:
    penalty = estimate_fuel_penalty("A320", 350.0, 350.0)
    assert penalty == {"delta_fuel_kg": 0.0, "delta_co2_kg": 0.0, "penalty_pct": 0.0}


def test_unknown_aircraft_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        estimate_fuel_penalty("A380", 350.0, 370.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        evaluate_altitude_candidates(0.85, 350.0, aircraft_type="A380")


def test_net_climate_benefit_positive_when_contrail_dominates() -> None:
    result = calculate_net_climate_benefit(
        avoided_contrail_ef_j=6.0e9, delta_co2_kg=30.0
    )
    assert result["net_benefit_positive"] is True
    assert result["benefit_to_cost_ratio"] > 1.0
    assert result["avoided_ef_j"] == pytest.approx(6.0e9)
    assert result["delta_co2_kg"] == pytest.approx(30.0)


def test_net_climate_benefit_negative_without_avoided_contrail() -> None:
    result = calculate_net_climate_benefit(
        avoided_contrail_ef_j=0.0, delta_co2_kg=30.0
    )
    assert result["net_benefit_positive"] is False
    assert result["benefit_to_cost_ratio"] == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_horizon_scaling_grows_co2_cost() -> None:
    short = calculate_net_climate_benefit(6.0e9, 30.0, metric_horizon_years=20)
    long = calculate_net_climate_benefit(6.0e9, 30.0, metric_horizon_years=100)
    assert long["benefit_to_cost_ratio"] == pytest.approx(
        short["benefit_to_cost_ratio"] / 5.0
    )
    assert long["net_benefit_positive"] is True


def test_climate_rejects_invalid_inputs() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        calculate_net_climate_benefit(1.0e9, 10.0, metric_horizon_years=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        calculate_net_climate_benefit(-1.0, 10.0)


def test_low_probability_abstains() -> None:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=0.20, baseline_fl=350.0)
    assert decision["status"] == "abstain"
    assert decision["recommended_fl"] is None
    assert decision["candidates"] == []
    assert decision["delta_fuel_kg"] is None


def test_high_probability_recommends_altitude() -> None:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=0.85, baseline_fl=350.0)
    assert decision["status"] == "eligible"
    assert decision["recommended_fl"] == pytest.approx(330.0)
    assert len(decision["candidates"]) == 2
    assert decision["delta_fuel_kg"] is not None
    assert decision["delta_fuel_kg"] > 0.0
    assert decision["delta_co2_kg"] is not None
    assert decision["delta_co2_kg"] == pytest.approx(
        decision["delta_fuel_kg"] * CO2_PER_KG_FUEL
    )
    assert decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] is not None
    assert decision["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] > 0.0
    for candidate in decision["candidates"]:
        assert candidate["rejected"] is False
        assert candidate["net_benefit_positive"] is True
    assert decision["candidates"][0]["target_fl"] == pytest.approx(370.0)
    assert decision["candidates"][1]["target_fl"] == pytest.approx(330.0)


def test_climb_above_ceiling_rejected() -> None:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=0.85, baseline_fl=390.0)
    climb = decision["candidates"][0]
    assert climb["target_fl"] == pytest.approx(410.0)
    assert climb["rejected"] is True
    assert climb["rejection_reason"] is not None
    assert "ceiling" in climb["rejection_reason"]
    assert climb["delta_fuel_kg"] == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert climb["net_benefit_positive"] is False
    assert decision["status"] == "eligible"
    assert decision["recommended_fl"] == pytest.approx(370.0)


def test_descent_below_minimum_rejected() -> None:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=0.85, baseline_fl=270.0)
    descent = decision["candidates"][1]
    assert descent["target_fl"] == pytest.approx(250.0)
    assert descent["rejected"] is True
    assert descent["rejection_reason"] is not None
    assert decision["recommended_fl"] == pytest.approx(290.0)


def test_activation_threshold_boundary_is_evaluated() -> None:
    decision = evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=0.40, baseline_fl=350.0)
    assert decision["status"] == "eligible"


def test_probability_validation() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=1.5, baseline_fl=350.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        evaluate_altitude_candidates(baseline_p_persist=-0.1, baseline_fl=350.0)
```

## tests/test_detect.py

```python
"""Tests for the classical satellite contrail detector.

Scenes are synthetic 100x100 split-window fields at 240 K with Gaussian
noise. A contrail is planted as a straight 20-pixel line with a +2.5 K
BTD anomaly; a cirrus-like cloud is planted as a round 20x20 blob with a
+3.0 K BTD anomaly.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest

from stratum.detect import btd as btd_module
from stratum.detect import filters

BACKGROUND_K = 240.0
SCENE_SIZE = 100
NOISE_STD_K = 0.2
PLANTED_ANGLE_DEG = 30.0
PLANTED_LINE_LENGTH_PX = 20
LINE_ANOMALY_K = 2.5
BLOB_ANOMALY_K = 3.0
BLOB_RADIUS_PX = 10


def _background_scene(seed: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Return a 240 K split-window background with independent Gaussian noise."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    bt108 = BACKGROUND_K + rng.normal(0.0, NOISE_STD_K, (SCENE_SIZE, SCENE_SIZE))
    bt120 = BACKGROUND_K + rng.normal(0.0, NOISE_STD_K, (SCENE_SIZE, SCENE_SIZE))
    return bt108, bt120


def _plant_line(
    bt108: np.ndarray, angle_deg: float, length_px: int, anomaly_k: float
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Add a straight line BTD anomaly of ``length_px`` pixels to ``bt108``."""
    start_col = (SCENE_SIZE - length_px) // 2
    cols = np.arange(start_col, start_col + length_px)
    rows = np.round(
        SCENE_SIZE / 2.0 + (cols - start_col) * math.tan(math.radians(angle_deg))
    ).astype(int)
    rows = np.clip(rows, 0, SCENE_SIZE - 1)
    bt108[rows, cols] += anomaly_k
    return bt108


def _plant_blob(
    bt108: np.ndarray, radius_px: float, anomaly_k: float
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Add a round circular blob BTD anomaly to ``bt108``."""
    grid_y, grid_x = np.mgrid[0:SCENE_SIZE, 0:SCENE_SIZE]
    mask = (grid_y - SCENE_SIZE / 2.0) ** 2 + (grid_x - SCENE_SIZE / 2.0) ** 2 <= radius_px**2
    bt108[mask] += anomaly_k
    return bt108


def _angular_distance(angle_a: float, angle_b: float) -> float:
    """Return the smallest angle difference in [0, 90] degrees."""
    difference = abs(angle_a - angle_b) % 180.0
    return min(difference, 180.0 - difference)


def test_compute_btd_is_temperature_difference() -> None:
    """BTD equals bt108 - bt120."""
    bt120 = np.full((4, 4), 240.0)
    bt108 = bt120 + 2.0
    btd = btd_module.compute_btd(bt108, bt120)
    assert btd.shape == (4, 4)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(btd, 2.0)


def test_compute_btd_rejects_shape_mismatch() -> None:
    """Mismatched channel shapes raise ValueError."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        btd_module.compute_btd(np.zeros((4, 4)), np.zeros((4, 5)))


def test_normalize_btd_constant_field_is_finite_zero() -> None:
    """A constant field normalizes to zero without division-by-zero artifacts."""
    normalized = btd_module.normalize_btd(np.full((30, 30), 240.0))
    assert np.all(np.isfinite(normalized))
    np.testing.assert_allclose(normalized, 0.0)


def test_normalize_btd_amplifies_spike() -> None:
    """A BTD spike stands out after background removal and robust scaling."""
    btd = np.zeros((31, 31))
    btd[15, 15] = 3.0
    normalized = btd_module.normalize_btd(btd)
    assert normalized[15, 15] > 10.0


def test_oriented_line_kernels_properties() -> None:
    """Eight zero-mean, odd-sized kernels with positive cores and negative flanks."""
    kernels = filters.build_oriented_line_kernels(length=11, width=3, num_angles=8)
    assert len(kernels) == 8
    for kernel in kernels:
        assert kernel.shape == (11, 11)
        assert kernel.sum() == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-12)
        assert kernel.max() > 0.0
        assert kernel.min() < 0.0
    assert np.array_equal(kernels[0], np.flip(kernels[0]))  # 0 deg is symmetric


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [0, 1, 2, 3])
def test_clear_sky_no_detections(seed: int) -> None:
    """Uniform 240 K background plus Gaussian noise yields no detections."""
    bt108, bt120 = _background_scene(seed)
    result = filters.detect_contrail_lines(bt108, bt120)
    assert result["num_detected"] == 0
    assert result["objects"] == []
    assert result["max_response_map"].shape == (SCENE_SIZE, SCENE_SIZE)


def test_straight_contrail_detected() -> None:
    """A 20-pixel +2.5 K line is detected with the correct orientation."""
    bt108, bt120 = _background_scene(seed=1)
    _plant_line(bt108, PLANTED_ANGLE_DEG, PLANTED_LINE_LENGTH_PX, LINE_ANOMALY_K)

    result = filters.detect_contrail_lines(bt108, bt120)

    assert result["num_detected"] >= 1
    assert result["num_detected"] == len(result["objects"])
    best = max(result["objects"], key=lambda obj: obj["length_px"])
    assert _angular_distance(best["angle_deg"], PLANTED_ANGLE_DEG) <= 15.0
    assert best["length_px"] >= 8
    assert best["mean_btd"] > 0.2
    assert abs(best["centroid"][0] - SCENE_SIZE / 2.0) < 10.0
    assert abs(best["centroid"][1] - SCENE_SIZE / 2.0) < 10.0


def test_round_blob_rejected_by_aspect_gate() -> None:
    """A round 20x20 +3.0 K blob fails the minimum aspect-ratio gate."""
    bt108, bt120 = _background_scene(seed=2)
    _plant_blob(bt108, BLOB_RADIUS_PX, BLOB_ANOMALY_K)

    result = filters.detect_contrail_lines(bt108, bt120)

    assert result["num_detected"] == 0, result["objects"]
```

## tests/test_diagnostics.py

```python
"""Tests for atmospheric diagnostics, aircraft parameters, and engine-aware SAC.

Covers the aircraft engine database lookup, the gradient Richardson
number (stability, turbulent shear, clamps, degenerate layers), the solar
zenith angle (equinox noon, night, seasonal high-latitude sun), and the
engine-dependent Schmidt-Appleman slope.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import datetime

import pytest

from stratum.physics import compute_sac_slope
from stratum.physics.diagnostics import (
    AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE,
    C_P,
    KAPPA,
    R_D,
    compute_richardson_number,
    compute_solar_zenith_angle,
    get_aircraft_params,
)

REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA = 23842.0


def test_aircraft_database_lookup_is_case_insensitive() -> None:
    assert get_aircraft_params("a320") == AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE["A320"]
    assert get_aircraft_params("A20N") == AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE["A20N"]
    assert get_aircraft_params("B738")["ei_n"] == pytest.approx(1.8e15)


def test_aircraft_database_fallback_to_default() -> None:
    params = get_aircraft_params("ZZZZ")
    assert params == AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE["DEFAULT"]
    assert params["eta"] == pytest.approx(0.33)
    assert params["q_fuel"] == pytest.approx(43.1e6)
    # Unknown types must not silently create database entries.
    assert "ZZZZ" not in AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE


def test_aircraft_database_entries_are_physical() -> None:
    for designator, params in AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE.items():
        assert 0.0 < params["eta"] < 1.0, designator
        assert 1.0e13 < params["ei_n"] < 1.0e16, designator
        assert 42.0e6 < params["q_fuel"] < 45.0e6, designator
    assert KAPPA == pytest.approx(R_D / C_P)
    assert KAPPA == pytest.approx(2.0 / 7.0, abs=1e-3)


def test_richardson_number_zero_shear_is_very_stable() -> None:
    ri = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=250.0,
        temp_below_k=240.0,
        u_above=25.0,
        u_below=25.0,
        v_above=-5.0,
        v_below=-5.0,
        alt_above_m=11000.0,
        alt_below_m=10400.0,
        p_ambient_pa=REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA,
    )
    assert ri == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_richardson_number_collapsed_layers_fall_back() -> None:
    ri = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=250.0,
        temp_below_k=250.0,
        u_above=40.0,
        u_below=0.0,
        v_above=0.0,
        v_below=0.0,
        alt_above_m=11000.0,
        alt_below_m=11000.5,
        p_ambient_pa=REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA,
    )
    assert ri == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_richardson_number_stable_stratification_is_positive() -> None:
    ri = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=221.0,
        temp_below_k=221.0,
        u_above=40.0,
        u_below=10.0,
        v_above=0.0,
        v_below=0.0,
        alt_above_m=11400.0,
        alt_below_m=10800.0,
        p_ambient_pa=REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA,
    )
    # Equal temperatures still give d(theta)/dz > 0 thanks to the pressure
    # term; moderate shear keeps Ri positive and below the clamp.
    assert 0.0 < ri < 10.0


def test_richardson_number_strong_shear_is_turbulent() -> None:
    ri = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=221.0,
        temp_below_k=221.0,
        u_above=60.0,
        u_below=5.0,
        v_above=20.0,
        v_below=-10.0,
        alt_above_m=11000.0,
        alt_below_m=10700.0,
        p_ambient_pa=REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA,
    )
    assert 0.0 < ri < 0.25


def test_richardson_number_clamps_extreme_instability() -> None:
    ri = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=200.0,
        temp_below_k=240.0,
        u_above=0.5,
        u_below=0.0,
        v_above=0.0,
        v_below=0.0,
        alt_above_m=11200.0,
        alt_below_m=10400.0,
        p_ambient_pa=REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA,
    )
    assert ri == pytest.approx(-5.0)


def test_solar_zenith_angle_equinox_noon_is_overhead() -> None:
    zenith = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
        datetime(2025, 3, 20, 12, 0, 0), lat_deg=0.0, lon_deg=0.0
    )
    assert 0.0 < zenith < 5.0


def test_solar_zenith_angle_night_is_below_horizon() -> None:
    zenith = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
        datetime(2025, 3, 20, 0, 0, 0), lat_deg=0.0, lon_deg=0.0
    )
    assert zenith > 90.0
    assert zenith <= 180.0


def test_solar_zenith_angle_winter_high_latitude() -> None:
    zenith = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
        datetime(2025, 12, 21, 12, 0, 0), lat_deg=60.0, lon_deg=0.0
    )
    # Solar altitude = 90 - 60 - 23.4 = 6.6 deg, so zenith ~ 83.4 deg.
    assert 78.0 < zenith < 88.0


def test_solar_zenith_angle_bounds_over_day() -> None:
    for hour in range(24):
        for lat_deg, lon_deg in ((19.09, 72.87), (28.57, 77.10), (-40.0, 150.0)):
            zenith = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
                datetime(2025, 6, 15, hour, 30, 0), lat_deg, lon_deg
            )
            assert math.isfinite(zenith)
            assert 0.0 <= zenith <= 180.0


def test_sac_slope_increases_with_engine_efficiency() -> None:
    """A more efficient engine leaves less waste heat: G rises with eta."""
    low_eta = compute_sac_slope(REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA, eta=0.30)
    high_eta = compute_sac_slope(REFERENCE_PRESSURE_PA, eta=0.40)
    assert high_eta > low_eta
    assert high_eta / low_eta == pytest.approx(0.70 / 0.60, rel=1e-12)


def test_sac_slope_scales_linearly_with_pressure() -> None:
    low_pressure = compute_sac_slope(20000.0)
    high_pressure = compute_sac_slope(40000.0)
    assert high_pressure == pytest.approx(2.0 * low_pressure, rel=1e-12)


def test_legacy_diagnostics_shim_still_importable() -> None:
    from stratum.diagnostics import get_aircraft_params as legacy_lookup

    assert legacy_lookup("A20N") == AIRCRAFT_ENGINE_DATABASE["A20N"]
```

## tests/test_features.py

```python
"""Schema tests for the extended 21-feature correction-model contract.

The engine-microphysics upgrade replaces the cyclical encodings and
derived temperature margins with the raw ``month``,
``local_solar_time_hours``, Schmidt-Appleman threshold temperatures
(``tlm_k``, ``tlc_k``) and the four diagnostic/engine features
(Richardson number, solar zenith, propulsion efficiency, log soot EI).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest

from stratum.model.features import (
    DEFAULT_ENGINE_EFFICIENCY_ETA,
    DEFAULT_LOG_SOOT_EI_N,
    DEFAULT_RICHARDSON_NUMBER,
    DEFAULT_SOLAR_ZENITH_DEG,
    FEATURE_NAMES,
    encode_cyclical,
    extract_features_row,
    feature_vector,
)

EXPECTED_FEATURE_NAMES = (
    "rhi",
    "rhi_above",
    "rhi_below",
    "rhi_grad",
    "temp_k",
    "tlm_k",
    "tlc_k",
    "pressure_pa",
    "cloud_ice",
    "high_cloud_fraction",
    "vertical_velocity_omega",
    "vertical_wind_shear",
    "dist_to_tropopause_m",
    "lat",
    "lon",
    "month",
    "local_solar_time_hours",
    "richardson_number",
    "solar_zenith_deg",
    "engine_efficiency_eta",
    "log_soot_ei_n",
)


def _base_kwargs() -> dict[str, float]:
    return {
        "rhi": 0.9,
        "rhi_above": 1.1,
        "rhi_below": 0.7,
        "rhi_grad": 0.4,
        "temp_k": 225.0,
        "tlm_k": 231.0,
        "tlc_k": 226.0,
        "pressure_pa": 25000.0,
        "cloud_ice": 1.0,
        "high_cloud_fraction": 0.3,
        "vertical_velocity_omega": -0.05,
        "vertical_wind_shear": 6.0,
        "dist_to_tropopause_m": 1500.0,
        "lat": 28.6,
        "lon": 77.2,
        "month": 12.0,
        "local_solar_time_hours": 1.0,
    }


def test_feature_schema_is_exact_and_ordered() -> None:
    assert FEATURE_NAMES == EXPECTED_FEATURE_NAMES
    assert len(FEATURE_NAMES) == 21


def test_extract_features_row_uses_diagnostic_defaults() -> None:
    row = extract_features_row(**_base_kwargs())
    assert tuple(row) == FEATURE_NAMES
    assert row["richardson_number"] == pytest.approx(DEFAULT_RICHARDSON_NUMBER)
    assert row["solar_zenith_deg"] == pytest.approx(DEFAULT_SOLAR_ZENITH_DEG)
    assert row["engine_efficiency_eta"] == pytest.approx(DEFAULT_ENGINE_EFFICIENCY_ETA)
    assert row["log_soot_ei_n"] == pytest.approx(DEFAULT_LOG_SOOT_EI_N)
    # Raw time and threshold features are carried verbatim.
    assert row["tlm_k"] == pytest.approx(231.0)
    assert row["tlc_k"] == pytest.approx(226.0)
    assert row["month"] == pytest.approx(12.0)
    assert row["local_solar_time_hours"] == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_extract_features_row_accepts_explicit_diagnostics() -> None:
    row = extract_features_row(
        **_base_kwargs(),
        richardson_number=0.35,
        solar_zenith_deg=145.0,
        engine_efficiency_eta=0.38,
        log_soot_ei_n=math.log10(4.0e14),
    )
    assert row["richardson_number"] == pytest.approx(0.35)
    assert row["solar_zenith_deg"] == pytest.approx(145.0)
    assert row["engine_efficiency_eta"] == pytest.approx(0.38)
    assert row["log_soot_ei_n"] == pytest.approx(math.log10(4.0e14))


def test_feature_vector_round_trips_in_canonical_order() -> None:
    row = extract_features_row(**_base_kwargs(), richardson_number=2.5)
    vector = feature_vector(row)
    assert vector.shape == (len(FEATURE_NAMES),)
    assert vector[FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi")] == pytest.approx(0.9)
    assert vector[FEATURE_NAMES.index("richardson_number")] == pytest.approx(2.5)
    assert vector[FEATURE_NAMES.index("log_soot_ei_n")] == pytest.approx(
        DEFAULT_LOG_SOOT_EI_N
    )


def test_feature_vector_rejects_missing_keys() -> None:
    row = {name: 0.0 for name in FEATURE_NAMES if name != "rhi"}
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        feature_vector(row)


def test_encode_cyclical_helper_is_kept() -> None:
    start = encode_cyclical(0.0, 12.0)
    wrap = encode_cyclical(12.0, 12.0)
    assert start[0] == pytest.approx(wrap[0], abs=1e-12)
    assert start[1] == pytest.approx(wrap[1], abs=1e-12)
    sin_jan, cos_jan = encode_cyclical(1.0, 12.0)
    assert sin_jan == pytest.approx(math.sin(math.pi / 6.0))
    assert cos_jan == pytest.approx(math.cos(math.pi / 6.0))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        encode_cyclical(1.0, 0.0)
```

## tests/test_io.py

```python
"""Tests for the real-data ingestion layer (Step 7)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import xarray as xr

from stratum.io import (
    ADSB_COLUMNS,
    ERA5_VARIABLES,
    SEVIRI_CHANNELS,
    clean_trajectory_points,
    crop_to_domain,
    interpolate_met_to_segment,
    load_era5_dataset,
)

# The netCDF4 1.7.2 wheel can emit a spurious NumPy binary-compatibility
# RuntimeWarning on first engine use under pytest; functionality is
# covered by the assertions themselves.
pytestmark = pytest.mark.filterwarnings(
    "ignore:numpy.ndarray size changed, may indicate binary incompatibility"
)

BASE_TIME = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01T06:00:00Z")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ADS-B schema and cleaning
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _record(**overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Build one raw ADS-B record with sensible cruise defaults."""
    record: dict[str, object] = {
        "icao24": "ABC123",
        "time": BASE_TIME,
        "lat": 20.0,
        "lon": 75.0,
        "baro_alt_ft": 35000.0,
        "geo_alt_ft": 35200.0,
        "gs_kt": 450.0,
        "track_deg": 90.0,
        "vrate_fpm": 0.0,
        "callsign": " AIC123 ",
        "on_ground": False,
        "source": "OpenSky",
    }
    record.update(overrides)
    return record


def _frame_from(overrides_list: list[dict[str, object]]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build a frame whose rows have distinct timestamps by default."""
    records = []
    for index, overrides in enumerate(overrides_list):
        merged: dict[str, object] = {
            "time": BASE_TIME + pd.Timedelta(minutes=index),
        }
        merged.update(overrides)
        records.append(_record(**merged))
    return pd.DataFrame(records)


def test_cleaning_drops_ground_low_altitude_and_missing() -> None:
    frame = _frame_from(
        [
            {},
            {"on_ground": True},
            {"baro_alt_ft": 10000.0},
            {"lat": None},
            {"lon": None},
            {"time": None},
            {"baro_alt_ft": None},
        ]
    )
    cleaned = clean_trajectory_points(frame)
    assert len(cleaned) == 1
    assert cleaned.loc[0, "icao24"] == "abc123"


def test_cleaning_enforces_indian_domain() -> None:
    frame = _frame_from(
        [
            {"lat": 45.0},
            {"lat": 5.5},
            {"lon": 100.0},
            {"lon": 67.9},
            {"lat": 6.0, "lon": 98.0},
            {"lat": 37.0, "lon": 68.0},
        ]
    )
    cleaned = clean_trajectory_points(frame)
    assert len(cleaned) == 2
    assert list(cleaned["lat"]) == [6.0, 37.0]


def test_cleaning_flags_implausible_vrate_and_speed_jump() -> None:
    t0 = BASE_TIME
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            _record(time=t0, icao24="a", gs_kt=450.0, vrate_fpm=0.0),
            _record(
                time=t0 + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1),
                icao24="a",
                gs_kt=620.0,
                vrate_fpm=0.0,
            ),
            _record(
                time=t0 + pd.Timedelta(minutes=2),
                icao24="a",
                gs_kt=450.0,
                vrate_fpm=7000.0,
            ),
            _record(
                time=t0 + pd.Timedelta(minutes=3),
                icao24="a",
                gs_kt=470.0,
                vrate_fpm=-500.0,
            ),
            _record(
                time=t0 + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1),
                icao24="b",
                gs_kt=620.0,
                vrate_fpm=0.0,
            ),
        ]
    )
    cleaned = clean_trajectory_points(frame)
    assert list(cleaned["icao24"]) == ["a", "a", "b"]
    assert list(cleaned["gs_kt"]) == [450.0, 470.0, 620.0]


def test_cleaning_normalizes_sorts_and_deduplicates() -> None:
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            _record(icao24="ABC123", time=BASE_TIME + pd.Timedelta(minutes=5)),
            _record(icao24="abc123", time=BASE_TIME),
            _record(icao24="ABC123", time=BASE_TIME),
            _record(
                icao24="DEF456",
                time=BASE_TIME - pd.Timedelta(minutes=1),
                callsign="  IGO999  ",
            ),
        ]
    )
    cleaned = clean_trajectory_points(frame)
    assert list(cleaned["icao24"]) == ["abc123", "abc123", "def456"]
    assert cleaned["time"].tolist() == [
        BASE_TIME,
        BASE_TIME + pd.Timedelta(minutes=5),
        BASE_TIME - pd.Timedelta(minutes=1),
    ]
    for _, group in cleaned.groupby("icao24", sort=False):
        assert group["time"].tolist() == sorted(group["time"])
    assert str(cleaned["time"].dtype) == "datetime64[ns, UTC]"
    assert cleaned.loc[cleaned["icao24"] == "def456", "callsign"].iloc[0] == "IGO999"
    assert set(cleaned["source"]) == {"opensky"}
    assert list(cleaned.columns) == list(ADSB_COLUMNS)


def test_cleaning_missing_required_column_raises() -> None:
    frame = pd.DataFrame([_record()]).drop(columns=["baro_alt_ft"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="missing required columns"):
        clean_trajectory_points(frame)


def test_cleaning_empty_result_keeps_canonical_columns() -> None:
    frame = _frame_from([{"on_ground": True}, {"baro_alt_ft": 9000.0}])
    cleaned = clean_trajectory_points(frame)
    assert cleaned.empty
    assert list(cleaned.columns) == list(ADSB_COLUMNS)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ERA5 ingestion and interpolation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

ERA5_LATS = np.array([30.0, 25.0, 20.0, 15.0])  # descending; loader must flip
ERA5_LONS = np.array([70.0, 75.0, 80.0])
ERA5_LEVELS = np.array([200.0, 250.0, 300.0])
ERA5_TIMES = np.array(["2025-01-01T00:00", "2025-01-01T06:00"], dtype="datetime64[ns]")


def _temperature(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return (
        200.0
        + 0.4 * (level - 200.0)
        + 0.2 * (lat - 15.0)
        + 0.05 * (lon - 70.0)
        + 0.3 * t
    )


def _specific_humidity(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return (
        1.0e-4
        + 1.0e-6 * (level - 200.0)
        + 1.0e-7 * (lat - 15.0)
        + 1.0e-7 * (lon - 70.0)
        + 2.0e-7 * t
    )


def _u_wind(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return 5.0 + 0.5 * (lat - 15.0) - 0.1 * (level - 200.0) + 0.2 * t


def _v_wind(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return -2.0 + 0.1 * (lon - 70.0) + 0.05 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.1 * t


def _omega(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return 0.01 * (level - 200.0) - 0.002 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.001 * t


def _cloud_fraction(level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t: float) -> float:
    return (
        0.1
        + 0.01 * (lat - 15.0)
        + 0.02 * (lon - 70.0)
        + 0.005 * (level - 200.0)
        + 0.01 * t
    )


def _expected_values(
    level: float, lat: float, lon: float, t_index: float
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Analytic field values used to verify exact linear interpolation."""
    return {
        "temperature": _temperature(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "specific_humidity": _specific_humidity(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "u_component_of_wind": _u_wind(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "v_component_of_wind": _v_wind(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "vertical_velocity": _omega(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "fraction_of_cloud_cover": _cloud_fraction(level, lat, lon, t_index),
    }


def _synthetic_era5() -> xr.Dataset:
    """Build a linear-in-all-coordinates ERA5-like dataset for testing."""
    level = ERA5_LEVELS[:, None, None, None]
    lat = ERA5_LATS[None, :, None, None]
    lon = ERA5_LONS[None, None, :, None]
    t_index = np.arange(ERA5_TIMES.size, dtype=float)[None, None, None, :]
    fields = {
        "temperature": _temperature(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "specific_humidity": _specific_humidity(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "u_component_of_wind": _u_wind(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "v_component_of_wind": _v_wind(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "vertical_velocity": _omega(level, lat, lon, t_index),
        "fraction_of_cloud_cover": _cloud_fraction(level, lat, lon, t_index),
    }
    target_shape = (
        ERA5_LEVELS.size,
        ERA5_LATS.size,
        ERA5_LONS.size,
        ERA5_TIMES.size,
    )
    data_vars = {
        name: (
            ("time", "level", "latitude", "longitude"),
            np.broadcast_to(field, target_shape).transpose(3, 0, 1, 2),
        )
        for name, field in fields.items()
    }
    return xr.Dataset(
        data_vars,
        coords={
            "time": ERA5_TIMES,
            "level": ERA5_LEVELS,
            "latitude": ERA5_LATS,
            "longitude": ERA5_LONS,
        },
    )


@pytest.fixture()
def era5_file(tmp_path) -> str:
    """Write the synthetic ERA5 dataset to a temporary NetCDF file."""
    path = tmp_path / "synthetic_era5.nc"
    _synthetic_era5().to_netcdf(path)
    return str(path)


def test_load_era5_dataset_validates_and_sorts_latitude(era5_file: str) -> None:
    ds = load_era5_dataset(era5_file)
    assert list(ds["latitude"].values) == pytest.approx([15.0, 20.0, 25.0, 30.0])
    assert set(ERA5_VARIABLES) <= set(ds.data_vars)


def test_interpolate_met_to_segment_matches_analytic_fields(era5_file: str) -> None:
    ds = load_era5_dataset(era5_file)
    values = interpolate_met_to_segment(
        ds, lat=22.5, lon=77.5, pressure_pa=22500.0, time_dt="2025-01-01T03:00"
    )
    expected = _expected_values(225.0, 22.5, 77.5, 0.5)
    assert set(values) == set(ERA5_VARIABLES)
    for name, expected_value in expected.items():
        assert values[name] == pytest.approx(expected_value, rel=1e-12, abs=1e-12)


def test_interpolate_accepts_tz_aware_datetimes(era5_file: str) -> None:
    ds = load_era5_dataset(era5_file)
    naive = interpolate_met_to_segment(
        ds, lat=20.0, lon=75.0, pressure_pa=25000.0, time_dt="2025-01-01T03:00"
    )
    aware = interpolate_met_to_segment(
        ds, lat=20.0, lon=75.0, pressure_pa=25000.0,
        time_dt=pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01T03:00:00Z"),
    )
    assert aware["temperature"] == pytest.approx(naive["temperature"], rel=1e-12)


def test_interpolate_outside_domain_returns_nan(era5_file: str) -> None:
    ds = load_era5_dataset(era5_file)
    values = interpolate_met_to_segment(
        ds, lat=5.0, lon=60.0, pressure_pa=22500.0, time_dt="2025-01-01T03:00"
    )
    assert math.isnan(values["temperature"])


def test_interpolate_rejects_bad_pressure(era5_file: str) -> None:
    ds = load_era5_dataset(era5_file)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        interpolate_met_to_segment(ds, 20.0, 75.0, -1.0, "2025-01-01T03:00")


def test_load_era5_dataset_rejects_missing_variable(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "missing_variable.nc"
    _synthetic_era5().drop_vars("temperature").to_netcdf(path)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="missing variables"):
        load_era5_dataset(str(path))


def test_load_era5_dataset_rejects_missing_coordinate(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "missing_coordinate.nc"
    _synthetic_era5().rename({"level": "unexpected_level"}).to_netcdf(path)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pressure level"):
        load_era5_dataset(str(path))


def test_load_era5_dataset_normalizes_cds_short_names(tmp_path) -> None:
    cds_style = _synthetic_era5().rename(
        {
            "time": "valid_time",
            "temperature": "t",
            "specific_humidity": "q",
            "u_component_of_wind": "u",
            "v_component_of_wind": "v",
            "vertical_velocity": "w",
            "fraction_of_cloud_cover": "cc",
        }
    )
    path = tmp_path / "cds_style.nc"
    cds_style.to_netcdf(path)
    ds = load_era5_dataset(str(path))
    assert "time" in ds.coords
    assert "valid_time" not in ds.coords
    assert set(ERA5_VARIABLES) <= set(ds.data_vars)
    values = interpolate_met_to_segment(
        ds, lat=22.5, lon=77.5, pressure_pa=22500.0, time_dt="2025-01-01T03:00"
    )
    expected = _expected_values(225.0, 22.5, 77.5, 0.5)
    assert values["temperature"] == pytest.approx(
        expected["temperature"], rel=1e-12
    )
    assert values["fraction_of_cloud_cover"] == pytest.approx(
        expected["fraction_of_cloud_cover"], rel=1e-12
    )


def test_load_era5_dataset_missing_file_raises(tmp_path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        load_era5_dataset(str(tmp_path / "does_not_exist.nc"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Meteosat SEVIRI cropping
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_seviri_channels_contract() -> None:
    assert SEVIRI_CHANNELS == ("IR_108", "IR_120", "IR_087")


def test_crop_to_domain_selects_subwindow() -> None:
    lats = np.array([30.0, 25.0, 20.0, 15.0])
    lons = np.array([68.0, 72.0, 76.0, 80.0])
    image = np.arange(16, dtype=float).reshape(4, 4)
    cropped, cropped_lats, cropped_lons = crop_to_domain(
        image, lats, lons, (26.0, 71.0, 19.0, 77.0)
    )
    assert list(cropped_lats) == [25.0, 20.0]
    assert list(cropped_lons) == [72.0, 76.0]
    assert cropped.shape == (2, 2)
    assert cropped == pytest.approx(np.array([[5.0, 6.0], [9.0, 10.0]]))


def test_crop_to_domain_preserves_descending_longitudes() -> None:
    lats = np.array([10.0, 20.0])
    lons = np.array([80.0, 76.0, 72.0, 68.0])
    image = np.arange(8, dtype=float).reshape(2, 4)
    cropped, _, cropped_lons = crop_to_domain(
        image, lats, lons, (25.0, 71.0, 5.0, 77.0)
    )
    assert list(cropped_lons) == [76.0, 72.0]
    assert cropped == pytest.approx(image[:, 1:3])


def test_crop_to_domain_empty_box_raises() -> None:
    lats = np.array([10.0, 20.0])
    lons = np.array([70.0, 80.0])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no pixel centers"):
        crop_to_domain(np.zeros((2, 2)), lats, lons, (15.0, 90.0, 12.0, 95.0))


def test_crop_to_domain_shape_mismatch_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not match"):
        crop_to_domain(
            np.zeros((3, 3)),
            np.array([1.0, 2.0]),
            np.array([1.0, 2.0]),
            (3.0, 0.0, 0.0, 3.0),
        )


def test_crop_to_domain_rejects_malformed_bbox() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        crop_to_domain(
            np.zeros((2, 2)),
            np.array([1.0, 2.0]),
            np.array([1.0, 2.0]),
            (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        )
```

## tests/test_match.py

```python
"""Tests for flight-contrail association, parallax, advection and labels."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest

from stratum.match import advect, label, observe, parallax

DELHI = (28.5, 77.0)
MUMBAI = (19.0, 73.0)
FL360_ALT_M = 36000.0 * 0.3048


def _haversine_km(
    coord1: tuple[float, float], coord2: tuple[float, float]
) -> float:
    """Reference great-circle distance between (lat, lon) pairs [km]."""
    lat1, lon1 = math.radians(coord1[0]), math.radians(coord1[1])
    lat2, lon2 = math.radians(coord2[0]), math.radians(coord2[1])
    a = (
        math.sin((lat2 - lat1) / 2.0) ** 2
        + math.cos(lat1) * math.cos(lat2) * math.sin((lon2 - lon1) / 2.0) ** 2
    )
    return 2.0 * parallax.EARTH_RADIUS_M * math.asin(math.sqrt(a)) / 1000.0


def test_parallax_fl360_delhi_displaced_north_east() -> None:
    """FL360 over Delhi shifts ~10-14 km away from the sub-satellite point."""
    delta_lat, delta_lon = parallax.calculate_parallax_displacement(
        DELHI[0], DELHI[1], FL360_ALT_M
    )
    assert delta_lat > 0.0  # northward, away from 0 deg N
    assert delta_lon > 0.0  # eastward, away from 45.5 deg E
    distance_km = _haversine_km(DELHI, (DELHI[0] + delta_lat, DELHI[1] + delta_lon))
    assert 10.0 <= distance_km <= 14.0, distance_km
    assert 0.07 <= distance_km / 111.32 <= 0.14


def test_parallax_mumbai_11000m_within_bounds() -> None:
    """11000 m over Mumbai shifts 8-15 km northeast as well."""
    delta_lat, delta_lon = parallax.calculate_parallax_displacement(
        MUMBAI[0], MUMBAI[1], 11000.0
    )
    assert delta_lat > 0.0
    assert delta_lon > 0.0
    distance_km = _haversine_km(MUMBAI, (MUMBAI[0] + delta_lat, MUMBAI[1] + delta_lon))
    assert 8.0 <= distance_km <= 15.0, distance_km


def test_parallax_zero_at_sub_satellite_point() -> None:
    """A target below the satellite has no parallax displacement."""
    delta_lat, delta_lon = parallax.calculate_parallax_displacement(0.0, 45.5, 11000.0)
    assert delta_lat == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9)
    assert delta_lon == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9)


def test_advect_eastward_wind_drifts_36km() -> None:
    """20 m/s eastward for 1800 s drifts the point ~36 km east."""
    new_lat, new_lon = advect.advect_segment(DELHI[0], DELHI[1], 20.0, 0.0, 1800.0)
    assert new_lat == pytest.approx(DELHI[0])
    assert new_lon > DELHI[1]
    assert new_lon - DELHI[1] == pytest.approx(0.3684, abs=0.002)
    distance_km = _haversine_km(DELHI, (new_lat, new_lon))
    assert 35.5 <= distance_km <= 36.5, distance_km


def test_advect_zero_wind_is_identity_and_negative_time_rejected() -> None:
    """Zero wind leaves the point unchanged; negative time raises."""
    assert advect.advect_segment(DELHI[0], DELHI[1], 0.0, 0.0, 3600.0) == DELHI
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        advect.advect_segment(DELHI[0], DELHI[1], 20.0, 0.0, -1.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("high_cloud_fraction", "viewing_zenith_angle_deg", "opaque_cloud", "expected"),
    [
        (0.6, 40.0, False, False),  # too much high cloud
        (0.2, 65.0, False, False),  # viewing zenith angle too large
        (0.2, 40.0, True, False),  # opaque cloud screens the scene
        (0.2, 40.0, False, True),  # all gates pass
        (0.49, 59.9, False, True),  # just inside the thresholds
    ],
)
def test_observability_gate(
    high_cloud_fraction: float,
    viewing_zenith_angle_deg: float,
    opaque_cloud: bool,
    expected: bool,
) -> None:
    """Observability requires cloud < 0.5, VZA < 60 and no opaque cloud."""
    assert (
        observe.is_scene_observable(
            high_cloud_fraction, viewing_zenith_angle_deg, opaque_cloud
        )
        is expected
    )


def test_tristate_label_matched_is_positive() -> None:
    """A matched segment is labeled 1.0 regardless of slot count."""
    assert label.assign_tristate_label(True, 0) == 1.0
    assert label.assign_tristate_label(True, 3) == 1.0


def test_tristate_label_observable_non_detection_is_zero() -> None:
    """Not matched with at least two observable slots is a hard 0.0."""
    assert label.assign_tristate_label(False, 3) == 0.0
    assert label.assign_tristate_label(False, 2) == 0.0


def test_tristate_label_unobservable_is_nan_not_zero() -> None:
    """Not matched with fewer than two observable slots is NaN, never 0.0."""
    for observable_slots in (0, 1):
        result = label.assign_tristate_label(False, observable_slots)
        assert np.isnan(result)
        assert result != 0.0


def test_match_requires_distance_and_angle() -> None:
    """Both centroid distance and line orientation must agree."""
    segment = (28.5, 77.0)
    heading = 175.0
    nearby_parallel = {"centroid": (28.52, 77.0), "angle_deg": 5.0}
    nearby_crossing = {"centroid": (28.52, 77.0), "angle_deg": 80.0}
    distant_parallel = {"centroid": (28.6, 77.0), "angle_deg": 175.0}

    assert label.match_segment_to_detections(segment, heading, [nearby_parallel])
    assert not label.match_segment_to_detections(segment, heading, [nearby_crossing])
    assert label.match_segment_to_detections(
        segment, heading, [nearby_parallel, nearby_crossing]
    )
    assert not label.match_segment_to_detections(segment, heading, [distant_parallel])
    assert label.match_segment_to_detections(
        segment, heading, [distant_parallel], distance_tolerance_km=12.0
    )


def test_match_angle_is_modulo_180() -> None:
    """Heading 350 deg and object angle 10 deg are 20 deg apart mod 180."""
    segment = (28.5, 77.0)
    obj = {"centroid": (28.5, 77.01), "angle_deg": 10.0}
    assert label.match_segment_to_detections(
        segment, 350.0, [obj], angle_tolerance_deg=20.0
    )
    assert not label.match_segment_to_detections(
        segment, 350.0, [obj], angle_tolerance_deg=19.0
    )
```

## tests/test_model.py

```python
"""Tests for the physics-constrained correction model (Step 5, extended schema).

The schema now carries raw threshold temperatures and the diagnostic and
engine features; dataset generators below build labels from the latent
temperature margin (``temp_k - tlm_k``) so the model must recover it from
the raw features.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest
from sklearn.metrics import brier_score_loss

from stratum.model import (
    FEATURE_NAMES,
    compute_brier_score,
    encode_cyclical,
    extract_features_row,
    feature_vector,
    fit_calibrator,
    train_contrail_model,
)

# LightGBM's sklearn wrapper validates feature names even when predictions
# receive plain ndarrays fitted from ndarrays; silence its spurious warning.
pytestmark = pytest.mark.filterwarnings(
    "ignore:X does not have valid feature names, "
    "but LGBMClassifier was fitted with feature names"
)

RHI_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi")
TLM_K_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("tlm_k")
TEMP_K_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("temp_k")
RICHARDSON_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("richardson_number")
ETA_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("engine_efficiency_eta")
LOG_SOOT_INDEX = FEATURE_NAMES.index("log_soot_ei_n")

_CONSTRAINED_INDICES = {
    RHI_INDEX: 1,
    TLM_K_INDEX: -1,
    RICHARDSON_INDEX: -1,
    ETA_INDEX: 1,
    LOG_SOOT_INDEX: 1,
}


def _synthetic_dataset(
    n_samples: int = 1500,
    seed: int = 7,
    nan_fraction: float = 0.0,
    label_noise: float = 0.05,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Physically shaped synthetic data consistent with the feature schema.

    Labels are drawn from a latent risk that increases with ice
    supersaturation and with a colder temperature margin
    (``temp_k - tlm_k``); the model sees ``temp_k`` and ``tlm_k``
    separately and must reconstruct the margin.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    features = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=(n_samples, len(FEATURE_NAMES)))
    rhi = rng.uniform(0.1, 1.5, n_samples)
    margin = rng.uniform(-12.0, 4.0, n_samples)
    tlm_k = rng.uniform(226.0, 234.0, n_samples)
    features[:, RHI_INDEX] = rhi
    features[:, TLM_K_INDEX] = tlm_k
    features[:, TEMP_K_INDEX] = tlm_k + margin
    logit = 2.5 * (rhi - 0.8) - 0.35 * margin
    prob = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-logit))
    labels = (rng.uniform(size=n_samples) < prob).astype(float)
    flipped = rng.uniform(size=n_samples) < label_noise
    labels[flipped] = 1.0 - labels[flipped]
    if nan_fraction > 0.0:
        n_nan = int(round(n_samples * nan_fraction))
        labels[rng.choice(n_samples, size=n_nan, replace=False)] = np.nan
    return features, labels


def _circle_distance(a: tuple[float, float], b: tuple[float, float]) -> float:
    """Euclidean distance between two unit-circle ``(sin, cos)`` points."""
    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])


def test_encode_cyclical_month_wrap_around() -> None:
    january = encode_cyclical(1.0, 12.0)
    december = encode_cyclical(12.0, 12.0)
    july = encode_cyclical(7.0, 12.0)
    expected = 2.0 * math.sin(math.pi / 12.0)
    assert _circle_distance(january, december) == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-12)
    assert _circle_distance(january, december) < _circle_distance(january, july)
    assert _circle_distance(january, july) == pytest.approx(2.0, abs=1e-12)


def test_encode_cyclical_rejects_nonpositive_period() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        encode_cyclical(1.0, 0.0)


def test_extract_features_row_assembles_schema_vector() -> None:
    row = extract_features_row(
        rhi=0.9,
        rhi_above=1.1,
        rhi_below=0.7,
        rhi_grad=0.4,
        temp_k=225.0,
        tlm_k=231.0,
        tlc_k=226.0,
        pressure_pa=25000.0,
        cloud_ice=1.0,
        high_cloud_fraction=0.3,
        vertical_velocity_omega=-0.05,
        vertical_wind_shear=6.0,
        dist_to_tropopause_m=1500.0,
        lat=28.6,
        lon=77.2,
        month=12.0,
        local_solar_time_hours=1.0,
        richardson_number=0.8,
        solar_zenith_deg=120.0,
        engine_efficiency_eta=0.38,
        log_soot_ei_n=14.6,
    )
    assert tuple(row) == FEATURE_NAMES
    assert row["tlm_k"] == pytest.approx(231.0)
    assert row["tlc_k"] == pytest.approx(226.0)
    assert row["month"] == pytest.approx(12.0)
    assert row["local_solar_time_hours"] == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert row["richardson_number"] == pytest.approx(0.8)
    assert row["solar_zenith_deg"] == pytest.approx(120.0)
    assert row["engine_efficiency_eta"] == pytest.approx(0.38)
    assert row["log_soot_ei_n"] == pytest.approx(14.6)
    vector = feature_vector(row)
    assert vector.shape == (len(FEATURE_NAMES),)
    assert vector[RHI_INDEX] == pytest.approx(0.9)
    assert vector[TLM_K_INDEX] == pytest.approx(231.0)
    assert vector[RICHARDSON_INDEX] == pytest.approx(0.8)


def test_extract_features_row_defaults_diagnostics() -> None:
    row = extract_features_row(
        rhi=0.9,
        rhi_above=1.1,
        rhi_below=0.7,
        rhi_grad=0.4,
        temp_k=225.0,
        tlm_k=231.0,
        tlc_k=226.0,
        pressure_pa=25000.0,
        cloud_ice=1.0,
        high_cloud_fraction=0.3,
        vertical_velocity_omega=-0.05,
        vertical_wind_shear=6.0,
        dist_to_tropopause_m=1500.0,
        lat=28.6,
        lon=77.2,
        month=12.0,
        local_solar_time_hours=1.0,
    )
    assert row["richardson_number"] == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert row["solar_zenith_deg"] == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert row["engine_efficiency_eta"] == pytest.approx(0.32)
    assert row["log_soot_ei_n"] == pytest.approx(15.0)


def test_feature_vector_rejects_missing_keys() -> None:
    row = {name: 0.0 for name in FEATURE_NAMES if name != "rhi"}
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        feature_vector(row)


def test_training_strictly_masks_nan_labels() -> None:
    features, labels = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=600, seed=11, nan_fraction=0.3)
    assert int(np.isnan(labels).sum()) == 180
    model = train_contrail_model(features, labels, list(FEATURE_NAMES))
    constraints = list(model.get_params()["monotone_constraints"])
    assert len(constraints) == len(FEATURE_NAMES)
    for index in range(len(FEATURE_NAMES)):
        assert constraints[index] == _CONSTRAINED_INDICES.get(index, 0)
    probabilities = model.predict_proba(features)[:, 1]
    assert probabilities.shape == (600,)
    assert np.all((probabilities >= 0.0) & (probabilities <= 1.0))


def test_training_rejects_all_nan_labels() -> None:
    features, _ = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=50, seed=5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        train_contrail_model(features, np.full(50, np.nan), list(FEATURE_NAMES))


def test_training_rejects_non_binary_labels() -> None:
    features, _ = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=60, seed=13)
    labels = np.zeros(60)
    labels[0] = 0.5
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        train_contrail_model(features, labels, list(FEATURE_NAMES))


def test_calibrated_probability_is_monotone_in_physics_features() -> None:
    features, labels = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=1500, seed=23, nan_fraction=0.1)
    split = 1000
    model = train_contrail_model(features[:split], labels[:split], list(FEATURE_NAMES))
    calibrated = fit_calibrator(model, features[split:], labels[split:])
    baseline = features[split].copy()

    def sweep(index: int, values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        probabilities = np.empty(values.size, dtype=float)
        for position, value in enumerate(values):
            row = baseline.copy()
            row[index] = value
            probabilities[position] = calibrated.predict_proba(row)[0, 1]
        return probabilities

    rhi_probs = sweep(RHI_INDEX, np.linspace(0.1, 1.5, 40))
    assert np.all(np.diff(rhi_probs) >= -1e-9)

    tlm_probs = sweep(TLM_K_INDEX, np.linspace(224.0, 236.0, 40))
    assert np.all(np.diff(tlm_probs) <= 1e-9)

    richardson_probs = sweep(RICHARDSON_INDEX, np.linspace(0.1, 4.0, 40))
    assert np.all(np.diff(richardson_probs) <= 1e-9)

    eta_probs = sweep(ETA_INDEX, np.linspace(0.30, 0.40, 40))
    assert np.all(np.diff(eta_probs) >= -1e-9)

    soot_probs = sweep(LOG_SOOT_INDEX, np.linspace(14.0, 16.0, 40))
    assert np.all(np.diff(soot_probs) >= -1e-9)


def test_brier_score_matches_sklearn() -> None:
    y_true = np.array([0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0])
    y_prob = np.array([0.10, 0.80, 0.65, 0.30, 0.90, 0.20, 0.55, 0.99])
    assert compute_brier_score(y_true, y_prob) == pytest.approx(
        brier_score_loss(y_true, y_prob), abs=1e-12
    )
    masked_true = y_true.copy()
    masked_true[0] = np.nan
    assert compute_brier_score(masked_true, y_prob) == pytest.approx(
        brier_score_loss(y_true[1:], y_prob[1:]), abs=1e-12
    )


def test_brier_score_rejects_shape_mismatch() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        compute_brier_score(np.array([0.0, 1.0]), np.array([0.5]))


def test_calibrated_probabilities_are_valid_and_skip_nan_rows() -> None:
    features, labels = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=400, seed=3, nan_fraction=0.2)
    model = train_contrail_model(features[:300], labels[:300], list(FEATURE_NAMES))
    calibrated = fit_calibrator(model, features[300:], labels[300:])
    proba = calibrated.predict_proba(features[300:])
    assert proba.shape == (100, 2)
    assert np.all((proba >= 0.0) & (proba <= 1.0))
    assert np.allclose(proba[:, 0] + proba[:, 1], 1.0)


def test_fit_calibrator_rejects_all_nan_labels() -> None:
    features, labels = _synthetic_dataset(n_samples=200, seed=17)
    model = train_contrail_model(features[:150], labels[:150], list(FEATURE_NAMES))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        fit_calibrator(model, features[150:], np.full(50, np.nan))
```

## tests/test_physics.py

```python
"""Baseline physics verification tests for Project STRATUM.

Asserts the STRATUM verification quantities for the Schmidt-Appleman
criterion at 250 hPa / eta = 0.30 and the ISA pressure conversion.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from stratum.physics import sac, thermo

P250_PA = 25000.0
ETA = 0.30


def test_mixing_slope_250hpa() -> None:
    """G at 250 hPa / eta = 0.30 equals 1.6413706426 Pa/K."""
    g = sac.G(P250_PA, ETA)
    assert round(g, 6) == 1.641371
    assert g == pytest.approx(1.6413706426, abs=1e-9)


def test_tlm_250hpa() -> None:
    """T_LM at 250 hPa / eta = 0.30 equals -41.9128742 deg C."""
    tlm_c = sac.solve_TLM(P250_PA, ETA) - 273.15
    assert tlm_c == pytest.approx(-41.9128742, abs=0.01)


def test_analytic_liquid_derivative() -> None:
    """Analytic d(e_w)/dT matches a central finite difference."""
    t_k = 231.0
    h = 1e-5
    finite_difference = (thermo.e_w(t_k + h) - thermo.e_w(t_k - h)) / (2.0 * h)
    assert thermo.d_e_w_dT(t_k) == pytest.approx(finite_difference, rel=1e-8)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("fl", "expected_hpa"),
    [(250.0, 376.015), (340.0, 249.996)],
)
def test_fl_to_p(fl: float, expected_hpa: float) -> None:
    """ISA pressure conversion checks for FL250 and FL340."""
    assert thermo.fl_to_p(fl) == pytest.approx(expected_hpa, abs=0.05)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("t_c", "rhi", "forms", "persists"),
    [
        (-52.0, 0.60, True, False),  # Point A
        (-50.0, 1.12, True, True),  # Point B
        (-38.0, 0.58, False, False),  # Point C
    ],
)
def test_sac_points(t_c: float, rhi: float, forms: bool, persists: bool) -> None:
    """SAC evaluation at verification points A, B and C (250 hPa)."""
    result = sac.evaluate_sac(P250_PA, t_c + 273.15, rhi, eta=ETA)
    assert result["forms"] is forms, result
    assert result["persists"] is persists, result
```

## tests/test_pipeline.py

```python
"""Tests for the end-to-end pipeline (Step 8).

Covers ISA pressure-altitude inversion, specific humidity to RHi
conversion, feature assembly along a synthetic Delhi-Mumbai trajectory
against an in-memory mock ERA5 dataset, and the corridor runner
(flagship decision cards, aggregation, and the no-risk path).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest
import xarray as xr

from stratum.model.calibrate import CalibratedModel, fit_calibrator
from stratum.model.features import FEATURE_NAMES
from stratum.model.train import train_contrail_model
from stratum.physics import e_i, fl_to_p
from stratum.physics import thermo
from stratum.physics.diagnostics import (
    compute_richardson_number,
    compute_solar_zenith_angle,
)
from stratum.pipeline import (
    HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA,
    evaluate_corridor_flight,
    extract_features_for_trajectory,
    pressure_to_altitude_m,
    specific_humidity_to_rhi,
)
from stratum.routes.corridor import AIRPORTS, great_circle_waypoints

# LightGBM's sklearn wrapper validates feature names even when predictions
# receive plain ndarrays fitted from ndarrays; silence its spurious warning.
pytestmark = pytest.mark.filterwarnings(
    "ignore:X does not have valid feature names, "
    "but LGBMClassifier was fitted with feature names"
)

MOCK_LATS = np.linspace(15.0, 32.0, 6)
MOCK_LONS = np.linspace(68.0, 82.0, 6)
MOCK_LEVELS = np.array([200.0, 225.0, 250.0, 300.0, 350.0, 400.0])
MOCK_TIMES = np.array(
    [
        "2025-01-01T00:00",
        "2025-01-01T01:00",
        "2025-01-01T02:00",
        "2025-01-01T03:00",
    ],
    dtype="datetime64[ns]",
)

_CRUISE_FL = 350.0
_NUM_POINTS = 25
_STEP_MINUTES = 6

#: Ground-truth logit used for the synthetic training set and the
#: scenarios (monotone increasing in RHi, decreasing in TLM margin).
_RHI_CENTRE = 0.85
_MARGIN_CENTRE = -4.0


def _mock_met(target_rhi: float, temperature_offset: float = 0.0) -> xr.Dataset:
    """Build an in-memory ERA5-like dataset with controlled mid-level RHi.

    Temperature is linear in pressure level (so interpolation is exact);
    specific humidity is prescribed from the ice saturation curve so that
    the vapor pressure corresponds to ``target_rhi`` at each grid level.
    """
    shape = (MOCK_LEVELS.size, MOCK_LATS.size, MOCK_LONS.size, MOCK_TIMES.size)
    level = MOCK_LEVELS[:, None, None, None]
    lat = MOCK_LATS[None, :, None, None]
    lon = MOCK_LONS[None, None, :, None]
    time_index = np.arange(MOCK_TIMES.size, dtype=float)[None, None, None, :] * 0.0

    temperature = (
        216.65 + 0.11 * (level - 200.0) + temperature_offset + 0.0 * time_index
    )
    saturation = np.array(
        [
            e_i(216.65 + 0.11 * (value - 200.0) + temperature_offset)
            for value in MOCK_LEVELS
        ]
    )[:, None, None, None]
    specific_humidity = target_rhi * saturation * 0.622 / (level * 100.0)

    u_wind = 8.0 + 0.05 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.004 * (level - 200.0)
    v_wind = -3.0 + 0.1 * (lon - 68.0) + 0.001 * (level - 200.0)

    dataset = xr.Dataset(
        data_vars={
            "temperature": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(temperature, shape),
            ),
            "specific_humidity": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(specific_humidity, shape),
            ),
            "u_component_of_wind": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(u_wind, shape),
            ),
            "v_component_of_wind": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.broadcast_to(v_wind, shape),
            ),
            "vertical_velocity": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.full(shape, 0.002),
            ),
            "fraction_of_cloud_cover": (
                ("level", "latitude", "longitude", "time"),
                np.full(shape, 0.20),
            ),
        },
        coords={
            "level": MOCK_LEVELS,
            "latitude": MOCK_LATS,
            "longitude": MOCK_LONS,
            "time": MOCK_TIMES,
        },
    )
    return dataset.transpose("time", "level", "latitude", "longitude")


def _trajectory(
    num_points: int = _NUM_POINTS,
    fl: float = _CRUISE_FL,
    start: str = "2025-01-01T00:00:00Z",
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build a cleaned ADS-B shaped VIDP -> VABB cruise trajectory."""
    waypoints = great_circle_waypoints(
        AIRPORTS["VIDP"], AIRPORTS["VABB"], num_points=num_points, fl=fl
    )
    start_time = pd.Timestamp(start)
    records = []
    for index, waypoint in enumerate(waypoints):
        records.append(
            {
                "icao24": "800abc",
                "callsign": "AIC866",
                "time": start_time + pd.Timedelta(minutes=_STEP_MINUTES * index),
                "lat": waypoint["lat"],
                "lon": waypoint["lon"],
                "baro_alt_ft": fl * 100.0,
                "gs_kt": 455.0,
                "on_ground": False,
                "source": "opensky",
            }
        )
    return pd.DataFrame(records)


def _trained_model(seed: int = 11) -> CalibratedModel:
    """Train a small calibrated baseline consistent with the scenario model.

    The latent label depends on the temperature margin ``temp_k - tlm_k``;
    the model sees ``temp_k`` and ``tlm_k`` separately.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n_train, n_val = 500, 300
    n_total = n_train + n_val
    features = np.zeros((n_total, len(FEATURE_NAMES)), dtype=float)
    rhi = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.6, n_total)
    margin = rng.uniform(-12.0, 4.0, n_total)
    features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi")] = rhi
    features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("tlm_k")] = 231.0
    features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("temp_k")] = 231.0 + margin
    logit = 3.0 * (rhi - _RHI_CENTRE) - 0.15 * (margin - _MARGIN_CENTRE)
    labels = (rng.uniform(size=n_total) < 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-logit))).astype(float)
    model = train_contrail_model(
        features[:n_train], labels[:n_train], list(FEATURE_NAMES)
    )
    return fit_calibrator(model, features[n_train:], labels[n_train:])


def _contiguous_flag_runs(segments: list[dict]) -> int:
    """Count contiguous runs of segments carrying a decision record."""
    runs = 0
    previous_flagged = False
    for segment in segments:
        flagged = segment["decision_status"] is not None
        if flagged and not previous_flagged:
            runs += 1
        previous_flagged = flagged
    return runs


def test_pressure_to_altitude_inverts_isa() -> None:
    assert pressure_to_altitude_m(thermo.P0_PA) == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-6)
    # FL350 in the ISA is 10 668 m (35 000 ft).
    altitude = pressure_to_altitude_m(fl_to_p(_CRUISE_FL) * 100.0)
    assert altitude == pytest.approx(10668.0, abs=5.0)
    # Monotone: lower pressure -> higher altitude.
    assert pressure_to_altitude_m(20000.0) > pressure_to_altitude_m(30000.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        pressure_to_altitude_m(-1.0)


def test_specific_humidity_to_rhi_roundtrip() -> None:
    temperature_k = 221.0
    pressure_pa = 23842.0
    for target in (0.3, 1.0, 1.4):
        humidity = target * e_i(temperature_k) * 0.622 / pressure_pa
        assert specific_humidity_to_rhi(
            humidity, pressure_pa, temperature_k
        ) == pytest.approx(target, rel=1e-3)
    assert math.isnan(specific_humidity_to_rhi(float("nan"), pressure_pa, temperature_k))


def test_extract_features_shapes_and_finiteness() -> None:
    features, metadata = extract_features_for_trajectory(
        _trajectory(), _mock_met(target_rhi=1.3)
    )
    assert features.shape == (_NUM_POINTS, len(FEATURE_NAMES))
    assert len(metadata) == _NUM_POINTS
    assert np.all(np.isfinite(features))

    pressure_pa = fl_to_p(_CRUISE_FL) * 100.0
    pressure_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("pressure_pa")]
    assert np.allclose(pressure_column, pressure_pa, rtol=1e-9)

    rhi_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi")]
    assert np.allclose(rhi_column, 1.3, rtol=0.1)
    gradient = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi_grad")]
    assert np.allclose(
        gradient,
        features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi_above")]
        - features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("rhi_below")],
        atol=1e-12,
    )

    eta_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("engine_efficiency_eta")]
    assert np.allclose(eta_column, 0.32)
    soot_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("log_soot_ei_n")]
    assert np.allclose(soot_column, math.log10(1.5e15))
    richardson_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("richardson_number")]
    assert np.all(np.isfinite(richardson_column))
    assert np.all(richardson_column > 0.0)
    zenith_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("solar_zenith_deg")]
    assert np.all((zenith_column >= 0.0) & (zenith_column <= 180.0))

    for info in metadata:
        assert info["rhi"] == pytest.approx(1.3, rel=0.1)
        assert info["flight_level"] == pytest.approx(_CRUISE_FL)
        assert info["pressure_pa"] == pytest.approx(pressure_pa, rel=1e-9)
        assert info["rhi_above"] - info["rhi_below"] != 0.0
        assert info["richardson_number"] >= 0.0
        assert 0.0 <= info["solar_zenith_deg"] <= 180.0
        assert info["engine_efficiency_eta"] == pytest.approx(0.32)
        assert info["log_soot_ei_n"] == pytest.approx(math.log10(1.5e15))
    assert any(info["sac_forms"] for info in metadata)
    assert all(info["ambient_ice_supersaturated"] for info in metadata)


def test_extract_features_wind_shear_matches_analytic_gradient() -> None:
    features, metadata = extract_features_for_trajectory(
        _trajectory(), _mock_met(target_rhi=0.6)
    )
    pressure_pa = fl_to_p(_CRUISE_FL) * 100.0
    offset_pa = HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA * 100.0
    delta_z = abs(
        pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa + offset_pa)
        - pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa - offset_pa)
    )
    delta_u = 0.004 * (-2.0 * HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA)
    delta_v = 0.001 * (-2.0 * HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA)
    expected_shear = math.hypot(delta_u, delta_v) / delta_z

    shear_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("vertical_wind_shear")]
    assert np.allclose(shear_column, expected_shear, rtol=1e-6)
    assert shear_column[0] > 0.0

    # Temperature confirms the linear-level interpolation used above.
    info = metadata[0]
    level_hpa = pressure_pa / 100.0
    assert info["temperature_k"] == pytest.approx(
        216.65 + 0.11 * (level_hpa - 200.0), rel=1e-6
    )
    assert info["tlm_c"] == pytest.approx(-42.5, abs=5.0)

    # Richardson number matches the analytic bounding-layer value.
    level_above_hpa = level_hpa - HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA
    level_below_hpa = level_hpa + HUMIDITY_OFFSET_HPA
    lat = float(metadata[0]["lat"])
    lon = float(metadata[0]["lon"])
    u_above = 8.0 + 0.05 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.004 * (level_above_hpa - 200.0)
    u_below = 8.0 + 0.05 * (lat - 15.0) + 0.004 * (level_below_hpa - 200.0)
    v_above = -3.0 + 0.1 * (lon - 68.0) + 0.001 * (level_above_hpa - 200.0)
    v_below = -3.0 + 0.1 * (lon - 68.0) + 0.001 * (level_below_hpa - 200.0)
    expected_richardson = compute_richardson_number(
        temp_above_k=216.65 + 0.11 * (level_above_hpa - 200.0),
        temp_below_k=216.65 + 0.11 * (level_below_hpa - 200.0),
        u_above=u_above,
        u_below=u_below,
        v_above=v_above,
        v_below=v_below,
        alt_above_m=pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa - offset_pa),
        alt_below_m=pressure_to_altitude_m(pressure_pa + offset_pa),
        p_ambient_pa=pressure_pa,
    )
    richardson_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("richardson_number")]
    assert np.allclose(richardson_column, expected_richardson, rtol=1e-9)

    # Solar zenith angle matches the astronomical computation.
    expected_zenith = compute_solar_zenith_angle(
        pd.Timestamp(metadata[0]["time_utc"]).to_pydatetime(), lat, lon
    )
    zenith_column = features[:, FEATURE_NAMES.index("solar_zenith_deg")]
    assert zenith_column[0] == pytest.approx(expected_zenith, rel=1e-12)
    assert 0.0 <= expected_zenith <= 180.0


def test_extract_features_validates_input() -> None:
    dataset = _mock_met(target_rhi=1.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        extract_features_for_trajectory(_trajectory().drop(columns=["lon"]), dataset)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        extract_features_for_trajectory(_trajectory().iloc[0:0], dataset)
    broken = _trajectory()
    broken.loc[0, "lat"] = float("nan")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        extract_features_for_trajectory(broken, dataset)


def test_runner_flags_moist_corridor() -> None:
    result = evaluate_corridor_flight(
        _trajectory(), _mock_met(target_rhi=1.3), _trained_model()
    )

    assert result["total_segments"] == _NUM_POINTS
    assert result["contrail_forming_segments"] >= 1
    assert result["contrail_persistent_segments"] >= 1
    assert result["flagged_segments"] >= 1
    assert len(result["recommended_reroutes"]) == _contiguous_flag_runs(
        result["segments"]
    )
    assert len(result["recommended_reroutes"]) >= 1

    for segment in result["segments"]:
        assert 0.0 <= segment["p_persist"] <= 1.0
        if segment["decision_status"] is not None:
            assert segment["decision_status"] in {
                "abstain",
                "eligible",
                "no_robust_candidate",
            }

    reroute = result["recommended_reroutes"][0]
    for key in (
        "event_index",
        "segment_index",
        "time_utc",
        "segments_in_event",
        "p_persist",
        "baseline_fl",
        "recommended_fl",
        "direction",
        "delta_fuel_kg",
        "delta_co2_kg",
        "avoided_contrail_ef_j",
        "benefit_to_cost_ratio",
    ):
        assert key in reroute
    assert reroute["p_persist"] >= 0.40
    assert reroute["baseline_fl"] == pytest.approx(_CRUISE_FL)
    assert reroute["recommended_fl"] != pytest.approx(reroute["baseline_fl"])
    assert reroute["delta_fuel_kg"] > 0.0
    assert reroute["delta_co2_kg"] > 0.0
    assert reroute["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] > 0.0
    assert reroute["benefit_to_cost_ratio"] > 0.0
    assert 1 <= reroute["segments_in_event"] <= _NUM_POINTS

    total = sum(
        entry["avoided_contrail_ef_j"] for entry in result["recommended_reroutes"]
    )
    assert result["total_avoidable_warming_j"] == pytest.approx(total, rel=1e-12)
    assert result["total_avoidable_warming_j"] > 0.0
    assert result["total_extra_fuel_kg"] == pytest.approx(
        sum(entry["delta_fuel_kg"] for entry in result["recommended_reroutes"]),
        rel=1e-12,
    )


def test_runner_dry_corridor_produces_no_advisories() -> None:
    result = evaluate_corridor_flight(
        _trajectory(),
        _mock_met(target_rhi=0.2, temperature_offset=10.0),
        _trained_model(),
    )

    assert result["total_segments"] == _NUM_POINTS
    assert result["contrail_forming_segments"] == 0
    assert result["contrail_persistent_segments"] == 0
    assert result["flagged_segments"] == 0
    assert result["recommended_reroutes"] == []
    assert result["total_avoidable_warming_j"] == 0.0
    assert all(segment["decision_status"] is None for segment in result["segments"])
```
